# Dynamics of Illusory Constructs

## 1. Question and Position

### 1.1 Research Question

This paper asks: how does a mere physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) become an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01)? The question is split into four:

1. What [function](concepts.md#c18) does a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) have: without it, which [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [never occur](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c01) (Section 4);
2. How a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is generated (Section 6);
3. Under what conditions a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) can be used (Section 7);
4. What happens after it is used (Section 8).

[Compression](concepts.md#c31) and being [commutative](concepts.md#c55), in Section 5, are the tools for answering the last three questions; Section 9 uses [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) as a control group.

The core is the second question. From mere physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) to a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) there are two steps: from dependence to [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), and from [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) to [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) (6.1).

This paper mainly discusses the [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03): calling a biological person a [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03) is done under the [configuration assumption](../paper_ae79e938_76e1_4bf9_b2ab_995c03e16c9e/concepts.md#c01), and there is no sharp boundary. This paper records the persons it discusses as [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and, by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50), assumes they are not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) (Section 10). The child in 12.1 is not assumed to be a [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03), and this is marked there. This paper does not assume that the constraints of different ranges are the same: the laws, currencies and institutions of different places can be entirely different; being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) within one range holds only for that range, and that two [cases](concepts.md#c24) use the same set of constraints is a condition to be stated separately (5.4, 5.5).

<note>

*This paper asks: when something merely makes a physical response, how does that become a "construct", that is, content that points at something and can be used to think things through and to tell others? It is split into four questions: what a construct is for, how it arises, under what conditions it can be used, and what happens after it is used. Section 5 deals with "compression" and "commutative", the tools for answering the last three questions; Section 9 uses today's artificial intelligence as a comparison. The heart of it is "how it arises": first there must be distinction, and then the content of the distinction is used to point at something; only then is it a construct.*

*This paper is mainly about the social person; who counts as one has no hard boundary and is the researcher's assumption. Every person discussed is treated as a Wo and assumed not to be locked in. The child in 12.1 is not counted as a social person, which is explained there. Also, this paper does not assume the rules are the same everywhere: what holds here cannot simply be carried over there.*

</note>

### 1.2 The Form of the Answers

**This paper's way of writing**: The answers this paper gives to the four questions are of only four kinds: pathways (if it happens, through what), necessary conditions (without them it [never occurs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c01)), no guarantee (even with all conditions present, it is not guaranteed to happen), and unconfirmability (if it happens, confirming it from outside is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02)). This paper does not state sufficient conditions.

Reasons: By the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25) and the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50), the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) cannot be uniquely derived in advance; by the definition of [empirical prohibition](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c51), conditions written in the form of [empirical prohibition](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c51) state only necessary conditions; by this paper's [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope (2.2), there is no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06), and a researcher's classing an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) as the subject's [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) made by treating the subject as another [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19), which by the definition of [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) may be misattributed; so confirming from outside whether a subject has made a given [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Note: Necessary conditions come from two sources: those derived from definitions are marked as conclusions; those calibrated from cases are marked as [empirical premises](concepts.md#c01). What this paper calls an [empirical premise](concepts.md#c01) is a premise obtained from cases or from existing tests, which new cases or tests can revise; among them, [Empirical Premises](concepts.md#c01) One to Three take the form of [empirical prohibition](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c51) and state only necessary conditions; [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Four registers the results of existing tests, and [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Five is a generalization induced from observations; neither is a necessary condition (Section 10).

<note>

*This paper never says "as long as these conditions hold, it will certainly happen." It says only four kinds of things: if it happens, which route it took; without what it will not happen; even with every condition present, it is not guaranteed to happen; and even if it happened, it is not guaranteed to be visible from outside. The reasons: what is not locked in cannot be predicted exactly at its next step; conditions written in the form of empirical prohibition serve only as necessary conditions; and all a researcher has is what the other did, so saying how the other drew lines inside may be wrong. Necessary conditions are of two kinds: those derived from definitions, and those generalized from cases that later cases may overturn; the latter are called empirical premises.*

</note>

### 1.3 The Base Is Unchanged; Three Layers Kept Apart

**The base is unchanged**: By the definition of [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04), a [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), result, and [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) again. The result becomes [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), later [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), and a new result falls out. [Weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) here is how heavy a [branch](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c05) is (the definition of [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06)), not desire, an intelligence score or a utility function, nor any parameter inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16). This paper adds no new base generative law for invention, [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13), [illusory constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), civilization or intelligence; all of them can serve as descriptions of patterns of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) under different [cuts](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), and their related [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) also enter later [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13).

**Three layers kept apart** (conclusion; the table below is called the [three-layer table](concepts.md#c58)):

| Layer | Content | Changes with engineering improvement? |
|---|---|---|
| [Theoretical limit](concepts.md#c02) | [Law of Flux](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c24), [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), [Law of Attrition](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c26), [Law of Non-Restoration](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c28) | Does not change |
| [Engineering limit](concepts.md#c03) | Reception and the five [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12) | May change |
| [History](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) and what actually falls out | Even with similar engineering architecture, different [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) and [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) may produce different [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) | Varies with the concrete [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); no unique result guaranteed |

Derivation: By their respective definitions, the [Law of Flux](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c24), the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25) and the [Law of Non-Restoration](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c28) hold for any [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) in the [universe](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c02), and the [Law of Attrition](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c26) holds for any [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19); none is limited by engineering conditions, so none changes with engineering improvement; an [engineering capacity](concepts.md#c12) is an indicator measuring a carrier (3.2), and when the carrier changes, the indicator may change; by the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), even with similar engineering architecture, the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) cannot be uniquely derived in advance.

The range engineering can realize is usually far smaller than the range physically possible; this is an intuitive judgment. This paper does not call it "a tiny fraction of the theoretically permitted range": such wording would need comparable state spaces and measures, which this paper does not give.

<note>

*At the very bottom there is only one thing: something arrives, it affects how heavy things are, a result falls out, and the result comes back again. This paper invents no new base rules for invention, culture or constructs; they are all just other angles for describing history. Beyond that, three layers are kept apart: the four laws are the theoretical ceiling and do not change however engineering advances; the senses and the five engineering capacities are the engineering ceiling and change with technology; what actually happens depends on each one's own history, so even with similar machines the results may differ. What engineering can do is probably far smaller than what is physically possible, but this is only an intuition; to state it as a precise ratio would need a comparable measure first, and this paper has none.*

</note>

### 1.4 The Researcher's Position

**Premise**: The researcher has no position outside [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13): a researcher proposing [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), publishing papers or rewriting definitions is producing [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); if they [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the object of study or at others, they likewise participate in later [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13).

**Premise**: This paper assumes that the researcher is a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19). This is an assumption, not a conclusion.

<note>

*The researcher is not someone watching from outside history. The concepts he proposes, the papers he writes and the definitions he changes are all things he does; when they reach others, they affect what comes after just the same. This paper also assumes that the researcher is a "self", that is, the kind of thing that defines itself by distinction. This is an assumption, not something proved.*

</note>

### 1.5 The Studied Wo Is Not Presupposed to Be a Self

By the definition of [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19), the condition for being a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19) is defining oneself by [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). The act of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) leaves a [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) in [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); but the boundary that "these are me" points to is not given by the physical world (2.1). So when "self" is used to describe or organize [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), it is an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). This paper treats it as what is to be explained, not as the starting point of explanation. The upstream papers take "self" as their starting point, and they hold within those papers; what this paper changes is the question, and it does not judge the upstream wrong. Upstream concepts whose definitions use "self" are used in this paper only on the researcher's side, or as an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) the researcher uses when attributing, and not as an antecedent of the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) being studied.

**Two questions about "self":**

**Question one: Can the researcher use "self" to [compress](concepts.md#c31) the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)?** Yes. Without a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), explaining one [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) means tracing back the whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); with one, a large amount of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) can be grouped into a few [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). By [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Five in Section 10 (induced from the cases observed so far), the [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that has a [self-maintained filtering boundary](concepts.md#c47) lean toward maintaining the inside of the boundary; when the researcher, having stated a [case](concepts.md#c24) whose [target](concepts.md#c25) is that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), summarizes these as "it is maintaining itself", and reads the [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) in which he makes this summary as turning the [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at him into a [representation](concepts.md#c34), then by the definition of [compression](concepts.md#c31) this is a [compression](concepts.md#c31); whether it is [commutative](concepts.md#c55) is judged by the criterion of 5.3 (5.6); it does not concern whether the object of the [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) exists, nor does it imply that the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has made a self-definition: its [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) fits the description the researcher makes with "self"; it does not operate according to "self".

**Question two: Does the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) itself define itself by [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18)?** [Distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is not in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) (2.2); the studied [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s self-report is an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), and the researcher's classing it as the other's [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) made by treating the other as another [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19), which may be misattributed. So this paper makes an assumption here:

> **[Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04)** (premise): Under a stated [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), for some [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is cut out, it is assumed that it defines itself by [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). This is not a theorem; confirming it from outside is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02) (1.2). When it lacks one of the necessary conditions listed in point 3 of 6.3, or when a mechanism is found proving that none of its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06) (9.3), this assumption does not hold for it. At what scale to cut belongs to the researcher's [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23); "self" is not among the conclusions that [recutting](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c22) guarantees unchanged (the definition of [recutting](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c22)), so under another [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) this assumption may fall on different [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), or on nested [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) at the same time, for example a person and each of his nerve cells.

Researchers usually assume "self" at the human scale, and the reasons are practical: most of the [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) a researcher receives [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the researcher at this scale; the asymmetry of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) caused by skin is obvious at this scale; and the researcher also draws himself at this scale, and the researcher's then reading the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of others as the [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) by which they draw themselves at this scale is itself a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02). These reasons do not make the human scale the "true self".

Whether the studied [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has [first-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c24) or [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) likewise depends on the [configuration assumption](../paper_ae79e938_76e1_4bf9_b2ab_995c03e16c9e/concepts.md#c01). [Second-order capacity](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c02) is itself an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) (the definition of [second-order capacity](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c02)).

<note>

*The upstream papers all start from "self"; this paper goes the other way and treats "self" as what is to be explained. The physical world will not draw the line of "these are me" for you, so "self" is a construct.*

*A researcher can use "self" to summarize what something does; for example, a cell keeps repairing its own membrane, so we say "it is maintaining itself". This is the researcher's simplification; it does not mean the cell has itself settled on a "self". As for whether a thing itself defines itself by distinction, that cannot be seen in the record, and what it says about itself is also just a sentence, so it can only be assumed, and assumed of something the researcher has cut out. Under another cut, the assumption may fall on a person, or on each of his nerve cells at the same time. Researchers habitually put "self" at the human scale only because it is convenient, not because the human is the "true self".*

</note>

### 1.6 Generalized Lossiness

**Conclusion**: By the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30), what falls on a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) is the part of one [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) that is for it, or the result of one [collapse](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c08). Another [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17) is the part of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) on which its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) depends, which by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) reaches back to [the initial state of the universe](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c14), including the results of many [writings](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) and [collapses](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c08); what one [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) brings to the receiver is only the part of one of those [writings](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) that is for it, or the result of one [collapse](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c08). So what one [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) brings to a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) is not the whole of another [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17); this does not depend on whether the receiver is a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19). This paper calls this point [generalized lossiness](concepts.md#c05). Upstream [lossiness](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c13), for one [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and when [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) is another [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), is the case where this generalization falls on a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19) (the definition of [lossiness](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c13)).

<note>

*Whatever single thing anything receives is never the whole of another Wo's own segment. The upstream said this only of "self"; this paper generalizes it to cases where the receiving side is not a "self".*

</note>

---

## 2. Inheritance and Terminology

### 2.1 Where Illusory Constructs Stand

**Two ways of appearing** (by the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01)): first, in the researcher's description, which uses a limited number of [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) to group a large amount of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) into fewer [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); second, in actual [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), for example names, [avowals](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c06), and classifications [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) in a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26). What can be registered is this physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) that can be confirmed, not that the speaker has, inside, an understanding of the same name and the same meaning. From a researcher's describing an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) with [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) A, it cannot be derived that the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) concerned has A; from the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) concerned saying A, it likewise cannot be derived that its inner [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) equals the A the researcher defined.

**One case of appearing inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)**: when the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) again through a [small loop](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c02) (2.3) and is used to point at some object and to organize its later [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), this content then fits the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). It can be regarded as a stand-in for [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) when that is not present; by the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), its material is only the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); if [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) is another [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), then by [generalized lossiness](concepts.md#c05) each of those [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) is not the whole of that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17); calling it a stand-in does not claim that what it stands in for exists.

**A [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) may use [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), or may not**: this concerns whether, at the moment the [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is made, existing [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are used as material. By 6.1, without [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) there is no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); so before the first [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) appears, the [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) it makes have no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) in their material that is the content of its own [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). Whether the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) later becomes a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is another matter; see 6.1.

**What the physical world gives is not boundaries but asymmetries of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10)** (conclusion): by the definitions of [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) and [individual](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c20), the same stretch of the [universe](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c02) may be drawn as one or as many, and either holds; grouping many things each drawn as one into a class is drawing once more on these boundaries. By the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), what [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) draws are all boundaries of this kind: which [presentations](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) are regarded as one, which stretch is regarded as the past, and whether a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) is regarded as the same one or two. Regarding a stretch as the past is regarding a stretch as one; regarding a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) as the same one is, by the definition of [identity](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c21), also regarding certain [presentations](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) as one. So the objects that the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) points to are none of them given directly by the physical world. But the physical world gives asymmetries of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), for example cell membranes and skin; some [cuts](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) happen to fall on such asymmetries, and so are especially useful.

**[Constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) and the physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that carries them**: the object of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is not required to have an independent physical entity, but the sounds, writing, gestures, containers and actions that carry it are all physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). A researcher's merely renaming a symbol does not change the physical [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) of the one studied; the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) concerned actually changing a sentence is another physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28).

**[Definitional Isolation and Renaming Invariance](concepts.md#c06)**: this paper records: an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) acquires theoretical meaning only through explicit definition, conditions of application, rules of combination and registrable criteria; natural-language names bring in no good or evil, purpose, causation, magnitude or psychological content.

<note>

*Constructs show up in two places: the researcher uses them to simplify a description; or they really appear in what is said aloud or written down. What a researcher can record is only that sentence or that document, not whether the speaker means the same thing inside.*

*Inside a thing, a construct appears like this: some content it once drew comes back to it and is used to arrange its later drawing and behavior. It is like a stand-in for something not in front of it; calling it a stand-in does not mean the thing stood in for really exists. At the moment of drawing, existing constructs may be used, or none at all, as when working by feel.*

*The physical world gives only the difference between "where things get through and where they do not", for example skin and cell membranes; it does not give the boundary "this is one, that is another". Boundaries are drawn, but some ways of drawing happen to fall on those differences and are especially useful. What a construct points to need not have substance, but the sounds, writing and movements carrying it are real physical events. A researcher renaming a word does not change what the studied one did; the studied one changing a sentence is a new event. The meaning of a word depends only on its definition, not on what its wording suggests.*

</note>

### 2.2 Terms and Notational Conventions

- **[Structure](concepts.md#c07)**: the usable and changeable parameters, connections, neural structures, dynamic states and the like on a carrier. The indicator that measures it is [structure capacity](concepts.md#c17) (3.2). This paper does not say "weight capacity", to avoid confusion with upstream [branch](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c05) [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06).
- At the engineering layer this paper does not use "weight", "computation", "configuration" or "update" for engineering things or acts, because these four words mean something else upstream; wherever the text links to upstream [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), it means the upstream sense (Proposition Five). A mapping that takes one or more [representations](concepts.md#c34) as input and yields a [representation](concepts.md#c34), such as transformation, combination, comparison or advancing, this paper calls an [operation](concepts.md#c08); the indicator that measures it is [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14) (3.2).
- "Writing" is used only in the upstream sense; content entering a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) this paper calls [explicit listing](concepts.md#c09).
- **[Signal containers](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) are not limited to writing** (premise): by the definition of [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26), the only conditions are not being a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and being [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) at every step from this [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) on; lasting is not a condition. On this basis, this paper records spoken words, broadcasts and television as [signal containers](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) once they have left the speaker. By the definition of [claimed boundary](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c10), [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) whose content is a classification include [avowals](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c06), declarations classifying people or things, and classifications written into a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26); the three are divided by different standards: [avowal](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c06) by object, classification declaration by content, writing into a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) by carrier; the three can overlap, and a spoken [avowal](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c06), once it has left the speaker, is also in a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26).
- **[Class illusory construct](concepts.md#c10)**: an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) obtained by further [lumping](concepts.md#c23) the results of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18).
- **[Record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope**: this paper records: only physical changes in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) are registered, not the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). [Distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is in the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) of the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that makes it, not in this paper's [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06).
- "Physical world" and "response" are used in their everyday senses. "Response" refers to the kind of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that the researcher does not class as [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18).
- When the engineering layer speaks of the one studied, the subject is "Wo", not "individual".
- "Construct" is short for [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). This paper does not use the word "concept" for a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) or a [representation](concepts.md#c34), to avoid bringing in essentialist presuppositions.
- When this paper says a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) performs [compression](concepts.md#c31), [operation](concepts.md#c08) or [inference](concepts.md#c36), it always means that the researcher reads its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) as [compression](concepts.md#c31), [operation](concepts.md#c08) or [inference](concepts.md#c36) (5.3, Proposition Five); when it says a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25)'s [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), it uses upstream [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05).
- "Self" in quotation marks means the name "self" as a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01); linked without quotation marks, it means upstream [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19).
- "Appear", "involve", "speed", "frequency", "given", "accumulate", "select", "update", "environment", "side", "one side", "boundary", "same" in uses such as the same one or the same set, the verb "decide", "conflict", "threshold", and "antecedent" when not referring to a [path](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c07) condition, are used in this paper in their everyday senses, not for the upstream concepts of the same name. "Slide rule", "calculator" and "calculation" in celestial mechanics are also everyday words.
- What this paper calls [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) means the class of [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) stated in 9.2.
- "Case" in uses such as saying that an [empirical premise](concepts.md#c01) is calibrated from cases and can be revised or overturned by new cases, "compare" in uses such as comparing a copy with [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) or comparison at the level of distributions, and the verb "represent" (as in "only represents" or "what is represented") and the verb "infer", are used in their everyday senses, not for this paper's concepts of the same name.
- "Cannot be derived" in this paper means: from the sources and [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) this paper lists, no unique result can be derived; it does not claim that nothing can be derived under any condition or from any material. "Not uniquely derived" is the same.
- Speech, names and quotations inside quotation marks are written as they are, without links; links are added only where this paper uses these concepts.

<note>

*This section sets out the wording. On the engineering side, a carrier's parameters and connections are called "structure"; taking one or more representations and producing a representation is called "operation"; the indicators measuring them add the word "capacity". Words the upstream has already used up, such as weight, computation, configuration and update, are not used by this paper for engineering things at the engineering layer; where the text really links to upstream "computation", it means the upstream sense. Saying a machine is "compressing", "operating" or "inferring" is always the researcher's reading of it.*

*Spoken words, once they leave the mouth, no longer change by themselves, so this paper counts them as signal containers too, not only written ones. Avowals, classification declarations and classifications written into containers are divided by different standards and can overlap. A construct obtained by grouping the results already drawn by distinction into a class is called a class illusory construct. The record notes only what people did, not how they drew lines inside.*

*There is also a string of words this paper uses only in their ordinary sense, such as "appear", "the same one", "who decides" and "threshold". "Cannot be derived" means that no definite answer can be derived from the material this paper has, not that no one could ever derive one. Words in quotation marks are written as they are, without links.*

</note>

### 2.3 Distinctions and Outputs Return to Oneself

**Premise**: This paper assumes that a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of it [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) itself before the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09), and participate in its later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06). Among upstream concepts, the [Wall of the Blind Spot](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c09) and the [Wall of Provenance](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c08) state that a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at oneself only for [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) whose object is oneself, and do not state that it does so before the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09); the definition of [small loop](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c02) describes this kind of [loop](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c31), but does not say that every [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at oneself through it. This paper generalizes it to all [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), as a premise.

<note>

*This paper assumes: every time a thing draws a line or does something, it comes back to the thing itself and affects it afterwards. The upstream said this only of "looking at oneself"; this paper makes it a general assumption.*

</note>

---

## 3. Engineering Capacities

> This section selects five [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12) as tools for describing a carrier. They are not necessary and sufficient conditions for a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), do not exhaust the capacities of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and are not further laws. The necessary conditions this paper states in terms of them are [Empirical Premises](concepts.md#c01) One to Three of Section 10. [Current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) and living things are only compared; it is not claimed that their inner mechanisms are the same.

<note>

*This section picks out five engineering capacities for describing a thing's "hardware". They are not conditions for being a Wo, not all of its capacities, and certainly not new laws. Necessary conditions written in terms of them are all generalized from cases and can be overturned. Comparing artificial intelligence with living things compares only the effects produced, not whether the mechanisms inside are the same.*

</note>

### 3.1 Limited Reception

**Premise**: Physical events in reality involve a great many different states, relations, scales and [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); any actual sense organ, sensor or performer of [compression](concepts.md#c31) can receive only part of them; for any [target](concepts.md#c25), the physical events involved in its [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17) cannot all be received either. Different modalities are not different base dynamics; they differ only in the source, form, measurable range and manner of representation of the signal. "Reality is nearly infinitely high-dimensional" is only an intuition: before a state space, a definition of dimension, a measurement window and encoding rules are specified, it is not a theorem.

**Conclusion**: Information that has not been obtained is not guaranteed to be reconstructible from existing input alone. The researcher also reads the [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) of the senses themselves as first selecting, [operating](concepts.md#c08) and [compressing](concepts.md#c31); "first" is an analytic distinction, not a fixed pipeline order.

**Conclusion**: How much capacity a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) needs, and whether this can be reduced to a few dimensions, cannot be derived: by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); by the definition of [impact](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c47), the effect of an [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) depends on the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) before it, and cannot be added up. A researcher's quantification of the capacity of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), as a statistical estimate of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), is, by the definition of [impression](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45), an [impression](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45), not a reading of the capacity itself.

<note>

*An enormous amount happens in reality, and eyes, ears and sensors each receive only a small part. Sight and hearing are not different physics, only different ways of receiving. Saying "reality has almost infinitely many dimensions" is only an intuition; you first have to say how it is measured before it counts.*

*What was not received is not guaranteed to be recoverable from what is at hand. The senses themselves also pick, calculate and compress first, and the order is only a way of speaking in analysis. How much capacity a thing needs, and whether it can be told in a few scores, cannot be derived either, because every arriving event affects it, and the effect depends on what it went through before. A researcher scoring capacities is making only his own estimate.*

</note>

### 3.2 Five Engineering Capacities

**Premise**: This paper describes a carrier by the following five [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12). Each is an indicator, measuring to what degree a carrier can perform a certain kind of act or a certain kind of retention; the acts themselves and the things on the carrier are referred to by this paper's other definitions (2.2, 5.3).

| [Engineering capacity](concepts.md#c12) | What it measures | What may happen when it falls short |
|---|---|---|
| **[Containment capacity](concepts.md#c13)** | Within a given structure, how many input differences and their relations can be distinguished, represented and handled at the same time | Different real conditions are mapped to the same [representation](concepts.md#c34) |
| **[Operation capacity](concepts.md#c14)** | Within limited resources and time, how many and how fast [operations](concepts.md#c08) can be performed on [representations](concepts.md#c34): transforming, combining, comparing, advancing | Delay, blocking, timeout, discarding data |
| **[Memory capacity](concepts.md#c15)** | How much earlier input, state or [operation](concepts.md#c08) results can be retained and later retrieved | Past differences cannot be retained or retrieved |
| **[Compression capacity](concepts.md#c16)** | When re-representing information with limited resources, how many of the differences needed for a specified use can be kept | Necessary differences are not kept; later discrimination or [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) becomes inaccurate |
| **[Structure capacity](concepts.md#c17)** | The degree to which existing [structure](concepts.md#c07) takes part in forming [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); with [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04), how much [structure](concepts.md#c07) can change with [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) and feedback | [Output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) does not meet the specified criterion; [structure](concepts.md#c07) becomes rigid |

Note: When what is measured is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the researcher's statistical estimates of these indicators are, by the definition of [impression](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45), [impressions](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45) (3.1).

<note>

*All five capacities are "indicators", just as shooting is an act and firepower is an indicator measuring shooting. Containment capacity: how many differences can be told apart and handled at once; operation capacity: how much and how fast it can calculate in limited time; memory capacity: how much of the past it can keep and later bring out; compression capacity: when re-representing with limited resources, how many of the needed differences it can keep; structure capacity: how the parameters and connections on the carrier take part in output and how much they can change. When one of them falls short, the troubles in the last column of the table appear. Measuring a Wo with these indicators yields only the researcher's estimate.*

</note>

### 3.3 Interacting, Not a Production Line

**Conclusion**: The five are not steps executed in sequence; they are not a linear chain. One mechanism can fall at once on aspects measured by several indicators, for example parameterized [compression](concepts.md#c31), or allocation of attention influenced by memory. This is an analytic relation, not the layer-by-layer execution flow of a particular network, and it does not replace the upstream definition of [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04).

<note>

*The five capacities are not an assembly line; one mechanism may involve several of them at once. This is only an analytic division, not the actual execution steps of some machine.*

</note>

### 3.4 What Can and Cannot Be Derived

- **Conclusion**: From the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16): the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) all participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), and at least at some [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12). This is dependence, not [representation](concepts.md#c34); it does not imply that there is any findable trace or [representation](concepts.md#c34) on the carrier.
- **Premise**: Under a specified [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), when the researcher describes the carrier state of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), if the carrier is finite, then in any stated finite description there must be cases where different [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) correspond to the same described state. This is a [compression](concepts.md#c31) the researcher makes at the layer of carrier description; it does not imply that those [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) no longer participate in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06).
- **Conclusion**: Whether a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) with some capacity below some degree will fail to persist within a stated time window cannot be derived: this paper does not specify which capacity, what degree, what external conditions and what time window, so it does not set this up as an [empirical premise](concepts.md#c01). By [conditioning on existing samples](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c14), the performance of those that persist is not enough to derive "the stronger the capacity, the better the persistence".

<note>

*The upstream guarantees only that everything a Wo has gone through affects it. This does not mean traces can be found on it. When a researcher describes its state in a finite way, different experiences will certainly be described as looking the same, but those experiences go on affecting it all the same. Whether something with too little capacity will fail to survive cannot be derived, since this paper does not specify which capacity, how weak, or in what environment. Looking only at those that survived, it also cannot be derived that the stronger the capacity, the easier the survival.*

</note>

### 3.5 Structure and Upstream Weight; the Bounds of "Correct"

**Conclusion**: Upstream [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) is how heavy a [branch](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c05) is (the definition of [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06)); engineering [structure](concepts.md#c07) is a summary of parameters, connections, dynamic states and changeability, and is not upstream [branch](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c05) [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), nor desire or purpose. This paper does not treat any engineering quantity as an already measured [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) of some [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

A wrong [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) may come from insufficient reception, lost [representation](concepts.md#c34), insufficient [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14), insufficient [memory capacity](concepts.md#c15) or improper [compression](concepts.md#c31); it does not necessarily come from [structure](concepts.md#c07). This paper calls an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) "correct" only after the task, the object of comparison, the observable conditions, the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) and the evaluation time window have been given. One correct answer does not establish an inner capacity of the same name, and one wrong answer does not settle which capacity fell short.

<note>

*The parameters and connections talked about in engineering are not the same thing as the upstream "weight", nor desire or purpose. This paper does not treat any engineering number as the already measured weight of some thing. An error may come from not receiving enough, a lost representation, being unable to calculate, being unable to remember, or compression gone wrong; it is not necessarily a problem of structure. To call an output "correct", you must first settle what it is compared with, how much error is allowed and how long you look; one right answer does not mean that capacity is really there, and one wrong answer does not show what was lacking.*

</note>

---

## 4. Question One: The Functions of Constructs

### 4.1 What Function Means

**Definition**: When this paper says that a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) has a certain [function](concepts.md#c18), it means that without this kind of [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), a certain kind of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [never occurs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c01). This states only a necessary condition; it does not mean a purpose, nor does it claim that with the [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that kind of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) will occur.

<note>

*Saying that a construct has some "function" says only this: without this kind of construct, certain things will not happen. It does not say the construct exists for the sake of this, nor that with the construct this will certainly happen.*

</note>

### 4.2 Constructs and Mere Distinction

**Conclusion**: By 6.1, a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), and it has an object it points to. When the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) only participates in later [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and is not used to point at some object, this paper calls it [mere distinction](concepts.md#c19), not a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). By the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) all participates in its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); this participation is not conditioned on an object pointed to. The difference between the two lies not in participation but in whether there is an object pointed to.

Calibration: A baker makes bread by feel, habit and experience: past experience participates in his present movements, the feel points at nothing, and this is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04); he may be unable to write an essay, and the bread is still good. When he wants to teach an apprentice, he writes the steps down in terms such as "proofing", "gluten strength" and "folding", each of which points at something: only then do the [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) appear in his [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). Before that, these [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) had not appeared in his [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); whether he had them inside cannot be derived from outside (4.3, note 5). Likewise: a person climbing a mountain walks toward that tree, and this is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); saying to a companion "that tree is our goal", even once, makes "that tree" a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). Walking along a minefield without stepping in is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); thinking at home "the minefield is east of the river", or telling someone "there are mines over there", is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).

The main conclusion of this question is the one above. The following three [functions](concepts.md#c18) are [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that exist only once there is an object pointed to.

<note>

*A construct differs from ordinary distinction in whether it "points at something". A baker kneading dough by feel has experience at work, but the feel points at nothing; this is only distinction. When he wants to teach an apprentice and writes down words like "proofing" and "gluten strength", each word points at something, and only then do constructs appear in what he writes and says; whether he had them in mind before that cannot be seen from outside. Walking toward that tree on a climb is distinction; telling a companion "that tree is the goal" makes "that tree" a construct. Both affect later behavior; the only difference is whether something is pointed at.*

</note>

### 4.3 Three Functions

**Conclusion**:

1. **[Reference](concepts.md#c20)**: without [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), it is impossible, when [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) is not present, to have a content point at it and take part in organizing [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (2.1). For a content to point at some object is to regard certain [presentations](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) as one object; by the definitions of [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) and [individual](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c20), this object is not given directly by the physical world, so when such content is used to organize [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), by the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) it is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).
2. **[Inference](concepts.md#c36)**: without [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), it is impossible to combine the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) with other content and derive new content about what has not yet [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10). The result of [inference](concepts.md#c36) points at [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) that has not yet [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10); without an object pointed to, there is no content that can be combined and carried forward. Reading a stretch of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) as [inference](concepts.md#c36) also uses the [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) of the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads it (5.3, Proposition Five).
3. **[Transmission between Wo](concepts.md#c21)**: without [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), among the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others, there is no [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) pointing at some object. What [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others is [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), which by the definition of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) may contain the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). By the definition of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), what [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others is the part of a [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) that falls on it as a [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30); by the definitions of [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) and [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30), the [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) left by a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) can also fall on others as a [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30), but what falls on others is this [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29), not the act of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). By the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), the material of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is only the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it, so a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) that others make from the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at them is their own [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). If names, marks and a patch on a map are read as [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that point at some object, then for the same reason, the objects they point to are not given directly by the physical world, and such [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) is, by the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) appearing in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28).

Notes:
1. **Participating and being used**: by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) all participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), and this participation need not be findable. [Mere distinction](concepts.md#c19) still leaves [writings](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) and still participates in later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); the baker's feel and the boundary of the minefield go on participating in that person's [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). What is missing without [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is not participation but pointing at some object.
2. **Correspondence at the engineering layer**: at the engineering layer, content that has an object pointed to corresponds to [representations](concepts.md#c34) that can [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) again and be processed (5.3). By the finite reception of 3.1, what a [representation](concepts.md#c34) produced by [compression](concepts.md#c31) is based on is not the whole of the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17); by the premise of 3.4, under a finite description of the carrier, the researcher reads content retained across [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) as a [compression](concepts.md#c31) at the descriptive layer. This is a correspondence, not a derivation: the two layers do not derive each other (5.1).
3. **Neither names nor number of times is a condition**: "you", "I" and "it" are not names of anything; the object pointed to differs each time, and what is pointed at is a position: the speaking side, the side spoken to, the thing pointed at. "That tree is our goal", said only once, is also a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) (6.1).
4. Once these [functions](concepts.md#c18) exist, whether [inference](concepts.md#c36) fits later [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) is another question, settled by being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) (5.3); a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) can go on being used even if it does not fit (8.2).
5. In the baker and climbing examples, the [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) appear in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) only when [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) is to [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others: nothing is said when one person works or walks alone, and they are spoken only when teaching someone or having a companion. [Constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) can be [transmitted between Wo](concepts.md#c21) through [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (7.1); but when nothing is said, by the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope (2.2) there is only [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06), and from outside it cannot be derived whether this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has this [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) inside; what can be determined is only whether it has appeared in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28).

<note>

*Only with constructs do three more things become possible: first, when something is not in front of you, you can still point at it to arrange behavior; second, contents can be combined to infer things not yet encountered, and even the number read off a slide rule counts as inference only when someone reads it as a number; third, words "pointing at something" can be passed to others. What is passed to others is always what is said or done; what others make of it is their own affair.*

*Without constructs, experience still works; what is missing is only "pointing at something". In engineering, representations that can be brought out and processed again and again roughly correspond to this kind of content, but this is only a comparison, not an inference. "You", "I" and "it" refer to positions, not names; a sentence said only once is still a construct. Whether an inference is right is another question; a construct that infers wrongly may still be used on and on. In the baker and climbing examples, constructs are spoken only when there is someone to teach or talk to; when nothing is said, one cannot tell from outside whether the person has the construct inside, only whether it has been said.*

</note>

---

## 5. Compression and Commutativity

### 5.1 Discrimination, Lumping and the Material of Compression

**Conclusion**: [Distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) adds one thing to mere dependence: drawing a boundary and regarding certain [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) as one (the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); this paper reads one [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) as: there are [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) on both sides of the boundary, and at least one side regards more than one [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) as one, a premise). This requires [discrimination](concepts.md#c22) and [lumping](concepts.md#c23) together: [discrimination](concepts.md#c22) is different [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) falling on different sides, and [lumping](concepts.md#c23) is different [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) falling on the same side. [Lumping](concepts.md#c23) is the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); [compression](concepts.md#c31) is the researcher's reading of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) (5.3); the two are not the same.

**Conclusion**: The two layers do not derive each other: a [representation](concepts.md#c34) is the content that an engineering-layer reading points to, while a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) at the content layer. From there being a [representation](concepts.md#c34) on the carrier, it cannot be derived that there is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01); from the researcher's attributing some [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), it also cannot be derived that a corresponding [representation](concepts.md#c34) can be found on the carrier. By 3.4, the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) does not imply that there is any findable [representation](concepts.md#c34) on the carrier; by note 5 of 6.1, whether a content has an object pointed to is the researcher's attribution, and may be misattributed.

**Conclusion**: [Compression](concepts.md#c31) brings no material of its own: the material that can be [compressed](concepts.md#c31) is limited by [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [containment capacity](concepts.md#c13); comparing and combining are limited by [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14); retaining and retrieving again are limited by [memory capacity](concepts.md#c15). This is not a sequence of production steps. On the assumption that other conditions are fixed and that raising one capacity does not harm the other indicators, the range of [representations](concepts.md#c34) that can be formed may expand; but whether a given [representation](concepts.md#c34) actually appears is still affected by the concrete [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), input and [structure](concepts.md#c07), and cannot be derived directly from the raising of capacity.

<note>

*Distinction must do at least two things: separate different things (discrimination), and also put different things together (lumping). Lumping is the content of a distinction; "compression" is the researcher's reading when looking at a stretch of process; the two are not the same thing. Engineering representations and content-level constructs also cannot be derived from each other: a representation in a machine does not mean there is a construct; a researcher saying someone has a certain construct does not mean a corresponding representation can be found in the machine.*

*Compression does not conjure material out of nothing: how much can be received, told apart, calculated and remembered all limit it.
