With higher capacity, more representations may be formable; but which one will actually appear still depends on concrete experience, and cannot be derived directly from capacity getting stronger.*

</note>

### 5.2 The Question

By 5.3, [compression](concepts.md#c31) turns [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) into a [representation](concepts.md#c34); by the finite reception of 3.1, [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) is not the whole of the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17), so what a [representation](concepts.md#c34) produced by [compression](concepts.md#c31) is based on is not the whole of the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17). [Compression](concepts.md#c31) takes only a part of reality. When the part taken is used for [inference](concepts.md#c36), why does it sometimes fit the later course of events and sometimes not? This section answers the question by writing a criterion in terms of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10).

<note>

*A representation produced by compression is turned out of the material received, and what is received was never the whole, so what it is based on is not the whole either. Compression only takes a part of reality, just as Newtonian mechanics keeps only mass, position and velocity and drops color. Then why does the part taken, when used to infer, sometimes fit what happens later and sometimes not? This section answers that question.*

</note>

### 5.3 The Criterion of Commutativity

**Definition**: Everything below is under a [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) stated by the researcher. Among them, [compression](concepts.md#c31) and [inference](concepts.md#c36) are readings the researcher makes of a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25)'s [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04); a [representation](concepts.md#c34) is the content these readings point to.

- **[Case](concepts.md#c24)**: a [target](concepts.md#c25), and two time windows: an [earlier window](concepts.md#c26) and a [later window](concepts.md#c27), the [earlier window](concepts.md#c26) first. The [target](concepts.md#c25) is a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04).
- **[Arrived material](concepts.md#c28)**: [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) about the [target](concepts.md#c25) that has [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the one performing [compression](concepts.md#c31). [Arrived material](concepts.md#c28) within the [earlier window](concepts.md#c26) is called [earlier material](concepts.md#c29); material of the same kind within the [later window](concepts.md#c27) is called [later material](concepts.md#c30). By the finite reception of 3.1, [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) is not the whole of the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17).
- **[Compression](concepts.md#c31)**: a [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) of a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) that the researcher reads as turning [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) into a [representation](concepts.md#c34). The [representation](concepts.md#c34) that actually falls out of [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) through [compression](concepts.md#c31) is called the [earlier representation](concepts.md#c32); what actually falls out of [later material](concepts.md#c30) through [compression](concepts.md#c31) is called the [later representation](concepts.md#c33). It is not required that the same material yields the same [representation](concepts.md#c34) every time.
- **[Allowed error](concepts.md#c56)**: an upper bound on the distance between [representations](concepts.md#c34), such that two [representations](concepts.md#c34) whose distance does not exceed it count as the same within this bound; the distance between two [representations](concepts.md#c34) is no more than the sum of the two distances by way of a third [representation](concepts.md#c34).
- **[Representation](concepts.md#c34)**: content that can [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) again, be processed, or be [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) in a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26).
- **[Retained difference](concepts.md#c35)**: when the [representations](concepts.md#c34) that actually fall out of two pieces of [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) through [compression](concepts.md#c31) differ beyond the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), this [compression](concepts.md#c31) is said to have retained the difference between the two.
- **[Inference](concepts.md#c36)**: a stretch of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) that the researcher reads as taking only a [representation](concepts.md#c34) and producing a new [representation](concepts.md#c34); if it also takes material other than the [representation](concepts.md#c34), it is not [inference](concepts.md#c36) on this [representation](concepts.md#c34).
- **[Inference result](concepts.md#c37)**: the [representation](concepts.md#c34) that [inference](concepts.md#c36) produces with the [earlier representation](concepts.md#c32) as material.
- **[Comparison](concepts.md#c38)**: whether the [inference result](concepts.md#c37) and the [later representation](concepts.md#c33) are the same within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56).
- **[Case attribution](concepts.md#c39)**: assigning the [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) and the [later material](concepts.md#c30) to the same [target](concepts.md#c25) is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18): by the definitions of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [individual](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c20), [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) regards certain [presentations](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) as one [individual](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c20). This [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is not obtained from the content of the [representation](concepts.md#c34), and is usually carried by [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) content such as a serial number. Judgments of being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) are relative to this [case attribution](concepts.md#c39).

**Definition** (criterion): [Commutative](concepts.md#c55) means that [inferring](concepts.md#c36) with a [representation](concepts.md#c34) in place of reality yields the same result as what [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) afterwards, that is, the [inference result](concepts.md#c37) and the [later representation](concepts.md#c33) are the same within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56); this can be judged for one [case](concepts.md#c24), for the registered [cases](concepts.md#c24), or for a stated range, which are, respectively, the following three:

- **[Commutative on a case](concepts.md#c40)**: the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) comes out the same.
- **[Commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41)**: for every registered [case](concepts.md#c24), the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) comes out the same.
- **[Commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42)**: the researcher states a set of [cases](concepts.md#c24) and claims that the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) comes out the same for every [case](concepts.md#c24). This is a claim.

The criterion uses only [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) and [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), and does not assume that any [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) can directly obtain the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17).

Note: If the one performing [compression](concepts.md#c31) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [representations](concepts.md#c34) that fall out of the same material may differ each time; this may make the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) come out differently, showing up as not [commutative](concepts.md#c55); by the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope of 2.2, the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) holds only [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), so these differences can be seen in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) only when they fall on [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28).

Calibration: Newtonian mechanics [compresses](concepts.md#c31) bodies by mass, position and velocity, and its [inference results](concepts.md#c37) agree with later observation; replace mass with color, and [inference](concepts.md#c36) can proceed just the same, but the results do not fit. Medical records: the records of two patients may be exactly the same in content; which patient is which is told by the record number, not by the content.

<note>

*Here a checking method is set out. Pick a thing (the target) and split time into an earlier and a later stretch. Compress the material received in the earlier stretch into a representation, infer from it, and get an inference result; then compress the material received in the later stretch into another representation, and see whether the two are the same within the allowed error. If they are, it is called "commutative": compressing first and then inferring gives the same result as waiting for things to happen and then compressing. Compression, representation and inference here are all the researcher's readings when looking at a stretch of process.*

*Whether the earlier and later material count as the same thing is drawn by the researcher, usually by a number rather than by content, just as two identical medical records are told apart only by the record number. One case fitting is called commutative on a case; every recorded case fitting is called commutative over registered cases; a researcher saying "this whole set fits" is a claim. Newtonian mechanics, with mass, position and velocity, calculates accurately; replace mass with color, and it can still calculate, but it does not fit.*

</note>

### 5.4 Propositions

**Proposition One: Lost Differences Cannot Be Recovered** (conclusion)

> If the [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) of two [cases](concepts.md#c24) actually yields the same [earlier representation](concepts.md#c32), while their [later representations](concepts.md#c33) differ by more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), and the two [cases](concepts.md#c24) use the same set of constraints, then [inference](concepts.md#c36) [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) on this [representation](concepts.md#c34) cannot be [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on both of these [cases](concepts.md#c24); for [inference](concepts.md#c36) not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on both of these [cases](concepts.md#c24) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: Under the conditions of the proposition, the [representation](concepts.md#c34) that the [inference](concepts.md#c36) actually takes is the same in the two [cases](concepts.md#c24). If this stretch of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), and the two [cases](concepts.md#c24) use the same set of constraints, then by the definition of [inference](concepts.md#c36) the [inference](concepts.md#c36) in both [cases](concepts.md#c24) takes only this [representation](concepts.md#c34), with no other material taking part; by the condition of the same set of constraints (10.1), the same input has the same [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) distribution; by the definition of [lock-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), only one result in this distribution has a nonzero [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), so the two [cases](concepts.md#c24) yield the same result; by the definition of the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) (5.3), the distance between two [representations](concepts.md#c34) is no more than the sum of the two distances by way of a third [representation](concepts.md#c34); if this result equaled both [later representations](concepts.md#c33) within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), the two [later representations](concepts.md#c33) would differ by no more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), contrary to the condition; if it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), that both results are the same, within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), as their respective [later representations](concepts.md#c33) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02). Sources: the definitions of [inference](concepts.md#c36) and [comparison](concepts.md#c38) (5.3); the condition of the same set of constraints (10.1); the definitions of [lock-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) and [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

**Proposition Two: However High the [Operation Capacity](concepts.md#c14), It Cannot Make Up Differences Lost in Containment** (conclusion)

> If the differences needed for some change are not contained and are mapped to the same [representation](concepts.md#c34), and the [later representations](concepts.md#c33) this change produces differ by more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), and the [cases](concepts.md#c24) use the same set of constraints, then however high the [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14), [inference](concepts.md#c36) [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) on this [representation](concepts.md#c34) cannot be [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on this change; for [inference](concepts.md#c36) not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on this change is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02). For [inference](concepts.md#c36) [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), only by obtaining material other than this [representation](concepts.md#c34), for example new [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), or other [representations](concepts.md#c34) retained earlier, is a remedy possible.

Derivation: "The differences needed for some change are not contained and are mapped to the same [representation](concepts.md#c34)" means that, of the two [cases](concepts.md#c24) that this change distinguishes, which the researcher can tell apart by their [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) and [later representations](concepts.md#c33), the [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) actually yields the same [earlier representation](concepts.md#c32); "[commutative](concepts.md#c55) on this change" means [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on both of these [cases](concepts.md#c24); this is exactly the condition of Proposition One. An [operation](concepts.md#c08) takes [representations](concepts.md#c34) as input (2.2), and [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14) measures how many and how fast [operations](concepts.md#c08) can be performed on a [representation](concepts.md#c34) (3.2); when [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14) rises, an [operation](concepts.md#c08) can map this [representation](concepts.md#c34) into a new [representation](concepts.md#c34), but the material is still only this [representation](concepts.md#c34) and the differences it did not retain cannot be recovered, and Proposition One applies directly: [inference](concepts.md#c36) [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) cannot be [commutative](concepts.md#c55), and for [inference](concepts.md#c36) not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) it is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02); material other than the [representation](concepts.md#c34) is not within the limits of Proposition One. Sources: Proposition One; the definitions of [operation](concepts.md#c08), [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14) and [containment capacity](concepts.md#c13).

Note: This is the first [engineering threshold](concepts.md#c53) in this paper derived from definitions. The five capacities cannot freely substitute for one another: differences lost in containment make a ceiling, not slowness.

**Proposition Three: [Comparison](concepts.md#c38) Reaches Only [Cases](concepts.md#c24) That Have [Arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10)** (conclusion)

> The [comparison](concepts.md#c38) any [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) makes reaches only those [cases](concepts.md#c24) whose [later representation](concepts.md#c33) has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it; this [later representation](concepts.md#c33) is what some compressor has made by [compressing](concepts.md#c31) the [later material](concepts.md#c30) that had already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the compressor itself, and the compressor need not be the [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) making the [comparison](concepts.md#c38). What the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) rests on is the [representation](concepts.md#c34) that [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) yields under [compression](concepts.md#c31), not the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17). If the range stated by the researcher includes [cases](concepts.md#c24) that have not yet [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), being [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) cannot be confirmed by [comparison](concepts.md#c38).

Derivation: When any [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) produces an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), what it takes is only what has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it; when the [later material](concepts.md#c30) has not yet [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at any [compressor](concepts.md#c31), there is no [later representation](concepts.md#c33) that can [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) making the [comparison](concepts.md#c38). By the finite reception of 3.1, [later material](concepts.md#c30) is not the whole of the [target](concepts.md#c25)'s [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17). Sources: the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); the definitions of [comparison](concepts.md#c38) and [later representation](concepts.md#c33) (5.3); 3.1.

Note: So a [compression](concepts.md#c31) being [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) is supported at most by the [cases](concepts.md#c24) that have [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10); new [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) may overturn it later. A [compression](concepts.md#c31) being [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41) only means that it matches the material that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10); whether it has caught the regularity of the [target](concepts.md#c25) or merely matches this material cannot be told from [comparison](concepts.md#c38). This paper does not deal with this problem. If the range is exactly the registered [cases](concepts.md#c24), what being [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) claims is exactly being [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41). This paper calls an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) "correct" likewise only after the task, the object of comparison, the observable conditions, the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) and the evaluation time window have been given (3.5).

**Proposition Four: For a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) Not [Locked In](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) Whose Differing Results Fall on the [Compared](concepts.md#c38) [Representation](concepts.md#c34), Being [Commutative](concepts.md#c55) in a Single Result Is [Not Guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02)** (conclusion)

> If the [target](concepts.md#c25) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and there are [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) between the [earlier window](concepts.md#c26) and the [later window](concepts.md#c27) at which it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), and among the results those [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) may produce there are two that differ by more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) in the [later representation](concepts.md#c33) being [compared](concepts.md#c38), then for any [inference](concepts.md#c36), being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) in a single result is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: By the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), no [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) in the [universe](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c02) can, before that moment, uniquely derive the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12); what can be derived is only [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06). By the definition of the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) (5.3), if one [inference result](concepts.md#c37) equaled two [later representations](concepts.md#c33) within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), the two would differ by no more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), contrary to the condition; so whichever result the [inference](concepts.md#c36) yields, at least one of these two possible results makes the [comparison](concepts.md#c38) come out different. Sources: the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25); the definitions of the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56) and [comparison](concepts.md#c38) (5.3); the definition of [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Notes:
1. When the researcher also makes a comparison at the level of distributions, for example comparing the inferred tendency with frequencies across many [cases](concepts.md#c24), such statistical estimates of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) are, by the definition of [impression](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45), [impressions](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45). By the definition of [generating function](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c15), [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) is determined by the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) up to that time; by the [Law of Flux](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c24), the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) differs at every [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) from before that [collapse](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c08). Nothing in the framework asserts that the distribution of [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) will recur, so recurrence of the distribution is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02), and being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) at the level of distributions is likewise [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).
2. Treating a concrete biological or social object as not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) follows the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50).
3. The difference between exact laws and statistical regularities lies in whether the object's next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), not in whether the researcher tries hard. By the definition of [random source](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c27), when it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), it too allows talk only of [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), so this difference is not limited to [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

**Proposition Five: A [Node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) Whose [Rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) Are [Computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) Need Not Make [Distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18)** (conclusion)

> For a [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) such as a slide rule, its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05). The researcher's reading this [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as [compression](concepts.md#c31) or [inference](concepts.md#c36) and reading the result as a number is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) made by the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads it, and the [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) it uses, if any, are that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). So a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) whose [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) need not make [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); the [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that [inference](concepts.md#c36) requires, and those that [compression](concepts.md#c31) uses if any, are with the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads it.

Derivation: By the definition of [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), a [locked-in round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c25) is [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05). By 5.3, [compression](concepts.md#c31) and [inference](concepts.md#c36) are the researcher's readings of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04): regarding a stretch of [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) as one [compression](concepts.md#c31) or [inference](concepts.md#c36) is drawing a boundary and regarding certain [presentations](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) as one, which by the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), and [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is an act of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16). The number read off is the content of this [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); by 2.1, the objects that the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) points to are none of them given directly by the physical world; if the number is used to point at some object and to organize the reader's later [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), then by the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) it is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). So what makes these readings and uses [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads it; a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) whose [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) need not make [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). Sources: the definitions of [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01); 2.1; 5.3.

Notes:
1. [Rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) that the researcher reads as [comparison](concepts.md#c38) may likewise be [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05); reading this [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as [comparison](concepts.md#c38), stating a range and claiming it [commutative](concepts.md#c55) is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of the researcher.
2. This proposition runs parallel to "culture is not conditioned on distinction" (7.6); for the control group, see 9.7.

**Proposition Six: [Self-Consistency](concepts.md#c45) and Being [Commutative](concepts.md#c55) Do Not Imply Each Other** (conclusion)

**Definition**: [Inference rules](concepts.md#c44): a description of [inference](concepts.md#c36), which can be [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) as a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14). [Self-consistency](concepts.md#c45): among the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44), no two give results on the same [representation](concepts.md#c34) that differ beyond the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56).

> That the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44) are [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45) does not imply being [commutative](concepts.md#c55); being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) also does not imply that the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44) are [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45).

Derivation: [Self-consistency](concepts.md#c45) concerns only the relations among the rules within the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44); being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) concerns [compression](concepts.md#c31) and later [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10). Neither definition refers to the other. By the definition of [comparison](concepts.md#c38), [comparison](concepts.md#c38) looks at the [inference result](concepts.md#c37) the [inference](concepts.md#c36) actually yields and the [later representation](concepts.md#c33); if another rule in the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44) gives a different result on the same [representation](concepts.md#c34) and the [inference](concepts.md#c36) did not proceed by that rule, that result does not enter the [comparison](concepts.md#c38), so being [commutative on a case](concepts.md#c40), [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41) or [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) does not exclude the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44) not being [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45). [Inference rules](concepts.md#c44) consisting of a single rule are necessarily [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45) by the definition of [self-consistency](concepts.md#c45); by Proposition One, when the [inference](concepts.md#c36) proceeding by this rule is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), it cannot be [commutative](concepts.md#c55) on both of two [cases](concepts.md#c24) whose [earlier material](concepts.md#c29) yields the same [earlier representation](concepts.md#c32), with the same constraints, and whose [later representations](concepts.md#c33) differ by more than twice the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56), and the definition of [self-consistency](concepts.md#c45) does not exclude such [cases](concepts.md#c24). Sources: the definitions of [self-consistency](concepts.md#c45) and [inference rules](concepts.md#c44); 5.3; Proposition One of 5.4.

Calibration: a mechanics using color as mass can be completely [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45) and yet not [commutative](concepts.md#c55); many practical rules of thumb are [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41) and yet inconsistent with one another.

<note>

*Proposition One: if earlier material is pressed into the same representation and later things part ways, far enough apart that no single result can fall within the error range of both, then an inference calculated rigidly by the same rules cannot be right on both sides, because the input and the rules are the same; for an inference not calculated rigidly, being right on both sides is not guaranteed either. Proposition Two: a difference that could not be told apart cannot be made up by an inference calculated rigidly by the same rules, however fast it calculates, and one not calculated rigidly can only get lucky; for one calculated rigidly, the only remedy is new material. So insufficient containment is a ceiling, not slowness. Proposition Three: only records already in hand can be compared, and such a record may have been written down by someone else who looked; what is not yet there cannot be compared. So saying a compression is commutative within a range only means it is supported so far by the cases already received. Its matching the material in hand does not mean it has caught the regularity; it may merely happen to match these. Comparison cannot tell the two apart, and this paper does not deal with it.*

*Proposition Four: if what is inferred about is something not locked in, for example a person, if the different results also differ enough on the representation being compared, whether a single result is accurate is not guaranteed to begin with; switching to statistical distributions is not guaranteed either, because the history it goes through differs at every moment, and nothing guarantees that the distribution will recur. Proposition Five: a slide rule only slides according to physical regularities, and that is "computation"; reading the slide as numbers, as multiplication, if constructs are used, it is the person holding it who uses them. So the slide rule itself need not have distinction. Proposition Six: a set of inference rules being free of internal contradiction does not mean it fits what arrives later; fitting does not mean being free of internal contradiction either.*

</note>

### 5.5 Boundaries Belong to Distinction; Arrival and Dependence Are Not Rewritten by Distinction; Scope

**Conclusion**: By 2.1, the physical world gives no boundaries, but gives asymmetries of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10). By the definition of [compression](concepts.md#c31), where a [compression](concepts.md#c31) cuts is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). By the upstream definitions, [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) is a [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) falling on a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04), and an [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17) is determined by dependence; and the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) changes views, not [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27). By the definition of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) is only appended to, so where a [compression](concepts.md#c31) cuts does not rewrite existing [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and dependence. So being [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) and "boundaries are not given by the physical world" do not conflict: the same stretch of the [universe](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c02) can have many [cuts](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23); whether a [compression](concepts.md#c31) under a given [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) is [commutative](concepts.md#c55) is judged by [comparison](concepts.md#c38), and does not conflict with boundaries not being given by the physical world. Nor does this conflict with "calling it a stand-in does not claim that what it stands in for exists" in 2.1: that concerns the object of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), while this concerns [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and dependence.

Being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) holds only within a stated range. For [cases](concepts.md#c24) outside the range the constraints can differ, and this paper does not assume they are the same as inside it (1.1); so from being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) within one range it cannot be derived that it is [commutative](concepts.md#c55) within another. By Proposition Three, the range itself is a claim.

Calibration: Newtonian mechanics becomes inaccurate when bodies move at speeds near that of light, because the relations at work there were dropped in [compressing](concepts.md#c31) by mass, position and velocity.

<note>

*Boundaries are drawn by people, but whether things fit depends on the actual arrivals and dependences; the boundaries people draw do not rewrite the arrivals and dependences that already exist. For example, the names "sun" and "planet" and their edges are drawn by people, but the sun's gravity affecting a planet's orbit is not drawn by people. So the two things do not contradict each other: the same stretch of the world can be cut in many ways, and whether a given cut fits after compression is known only by comparing. And it counts only within the agreed range; under another range it may become inaccurate, as Newtonian mechanics does near the speed of light.*

</note>

### 5.6 Application: The Researcher's Compression with "Self"

**Conclusion**: When the researcher has stated a [case](concepts.md#c24) whose [target](concepts.md#c25) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and reads the [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) in which he describes that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) with [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) such as "self" as turning the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at him into a [representation](concepts.md#c34) written with these [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), then by the definition of [compression](concepts.md#c31) this is one [compression](concepts.md#c31); whether it is [commutative](concepts.md#c55) is a separate matter, to be tested by the criterion of 5.3. For example, from "it is maintaining itself", [infer](concepts.md#c36) its [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of repairing the boundary and regulating exchange between inside and outside, and then [compare](concepts.md#c38) them with the later [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06). If "inside the boundary" does not coincide with the [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17), this [compression](concepts.md#c31) may fail to fit on some [cases](concepts.md#c24); whether it is not [commutative](concepts.md#c55) still depends only on [comparison](concepts.md#c38): it can be judged only once the non-coinciding part makes a difference in the later [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06).

Calibration: viruses borrowing a cell's machinery to replicate; autoimmunity.

<note>

*A researcher saying "this cell is maintaining itself" is a compression when saying it is read as turning what has been received into a statement; whether it fits must be checked separately. If the "self" the researcher draws does not match the range that actually affects it, the statement may fail to fit, but this can be seen only once the mismatch shows up in the record. For example, before a virus comes in, the statement keeps fitting; only after the virus comes in and borrows the cell's machinery does it fail to fit. The immune system attacking itself is the same.*

</note>

---

## 6. Question Two: How Illusory Constructs Are Generated

### 6.1 Two Steps: From Dependence to Distinction, From Distinction to Construct

**Conclusion 6.1**: From dependence to [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) there are two steps: from dependence to [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), and from [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) to [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). Without [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), there is no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); from there being [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), it does not follow that there is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).

Derivation: By the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) has an object it points to, and this object is not given by the physical world. So without [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), there is no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). When the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) only participates in later [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and is not used to point at some object, it does not fit this definition; by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) all participates in its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), and this participation itself has no object. So from there being [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), it does not follow that there is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). Sources: the definitions of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

Notes:
1. This conclusion agrees with "a distinction may use constructs, or may not" in 2.1: that concerns whether, at the moment the [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is made, existing [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are used as material.
2. Neither number of times nor names is a condition. Two common manifestations of [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01): pointing at what is not present (2.1), and appearing in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that is spoken or written down (the second way of appearing listed in the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01)).
3. Merely treating different things as one class is [lumping](concepts.md#c23) within [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (5.1), not a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). A baker judging different doughs "ready" every day is [lumping](concepts.md#c23) plus [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04) (4.2).
4. What this paper calls abstraction means that a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) has an object it points to and can come away from what is in front of one; it does not mean grouping different things into one class.
5. Whether a content has an object pointed to is the researcher's attribution: the content is the content of some [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of the object, so judging whether it has an object pointed to first requires assigning some [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of the object to this [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) contains only [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (2.2), so this assignment is made on the basis of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), is a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) (1.2), and may be misattributed. What the upstream guarantees is only participation in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), this participation need not be findable.
6. [Mere distinction](concepts.md#c19) still leaves [writings](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) and participates in later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) (4.3, note 1).

Calibration:
- Walking along a minefield, never stepping in at any step: the boundary takes part in action and has no object pointed to; this is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). Drawing a patch on a map, thinking at home "the minefield is east of the river, so crossing means a detour", telling someone "there are mines over there": "minefield" points at an object; this is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).
- Recognizing one's own dog every day: [lumping](concepts.md#c23) plus the participation of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); this is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). Calling it "Blackie" and asking "where did Blackie go": pointing at an object; this is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).
- "You", "I", "it": what is pointed to is a position, and the object differs each time; once spoken, it is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). "I went climbing yesterday" points at yesterday's self, who is not present; treating the self at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) as the same one corresponds to [cross-moment lumping](concepts.md#c49) on the [self ladder](concepts.md#c46) of 6.3.
- The baker and the climb: see 4.2.

<note>

*From a mere physical response to a construct takes two steps: first there must be distinction, and then the content of the distinction is used to point at something. So without distinction there is certainly no construct that is the content of its distinction, while with distinction there is not necessarily a construct. Grouping different things into one class does not yet count; it counts only when it "points"; what this paper calls "abstraction" is being able to point at what is not in front of one. A sentence said once, or something with no name, can still be a construct. Whether a content points at something is the researcher's judgment, and may be wrong.*

*Examples: walking along a minefield without stepping in is distinction; telling someone "there are mines over there" is a construct. Recognizing your own dog every day is distinction; calling it "Blackie" is a construct. "I went climbing yesterday" points at yesterday's self, and is also a construct.*

</note>

### 6.2 The Pathway of Generation

**Conclusion**: If the content of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) becomes an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), it can go through the following pathway: a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) draws a boundary, in which [lumping](concepts.md#c23) regards certain [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) as one; this [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) leaves a [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29), and the [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) through a [small loop](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c02) at the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that made it; the content of this [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is used to point at some object, for example pointing at it when it is not present, or appearing in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that is spoken or written down; later [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) take it as material and may [lump](concepts.md#c23) again. A [class illusory construct](concepts.md#c10) is a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) obtained by further [lumping](concepts.md#c23) the results of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), for example the "we" into which many "I"s are grouped.

Derivation: The material of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is only the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) before it, including one's own past [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); the premise of 2.3); this step follows 5.1 and 6.1; every round is an existing [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04), and no further generative law is added (1.3).

Notes:
1. The material can come from outside. If names and classifications in others' [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), they can be its material; but the [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) made with this material is its own [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); 4.3, point 3). The [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) of a name is not the transfer of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).
2. What is said here is "if it is generated, through what"; it does not claim that all the steps must be gone through, nor that the order is fixed.

<note>

*If content a thing draws itself becomes a construct, it goes roughly like this: first a line is drawn once, regarding certain things as one; this way of drawing comes back to oneself; its content is used to point at something, for example what is not in front of one, or it is said aloud; afterwards it is taken as material and grouped once more. A construct obtained by grouping things already drawn into a class, for example the "we" into which many "I"s are grouped, is called the "class of things" kind of construct. The material may come from others, for example a name one hears; but what is drawn with it is one's own drawing. Hearing a name is not having a construct handed to you. This says only "if it arises, through what", not that it must go through in this order.*

</note>

### 6.3 Necessary Conditions

1. **[Illusory constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) in general** (conclusion, by 6.1): without [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), there is no [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); when the content of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) only participates in later [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and is not used to point at some object, that content is not an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).
2. **[Class illusory constructs](concepts.md#c10)** (conclusion, by the definition of [class illusory construct](concepts.md#c10), 2.2): without further [lumping](concepts.md#c23) the results of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), there is no [class illusory construct](concepts.md#c10).
3. **Necessary conditions of the [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04)** ([empirical premises](concepts.md#c01)):
   - Without retaining some copy of its own [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (belonging to [memory capacity](concepts.md#c15)), or without checking the copy against subsequent [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) (belonging to [operation capacity](concepts.md#c14)), there is no [self-other discrimination](concepts.md#c48) (Section 10, [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) One);
   - Without retention across different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) (belonging to [memory capacity](concepts.md#c15)), or without [compressing](concepts.md#c31) the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) caused by oneself at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) into one class (belonging to [compression capacity](concepts.md#c16)), there is no [cross-moment lumping](concepts.md#c49) (Section 10, [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Two).

Note: The upstream guarantees only that [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), not that traces remain on the carrier (3.4). Point 3 is written in the form of [empirical prohibition](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c51), stating only that without these it [never occurs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c01), not that with them it occurs; they are calibrated from cases, and new cases may revise them.

**[Self ladder](concepts.md#c46)**: the rungs below state only necessary conditions. In question two of 1.5, they are used only to negate the [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04); having all of them only means that this assumption has not been negated. The names of the rungs at the content layer ([discrimination](concepts.md#c22), [lumping](concepts.md#c23), identity) are the researcher's attributions.

```text
Wo (own segment, determined by dependence)
└─ Self-maintained filtering boundary ... physical asymmetry of arrival, needs no distinction (foundation of question one)
   └─ Distinction (discrimination + lumping)
      └─ Self-other discrimination ............ necessary condition of the momentary self
         └─ Cross-moment lumping, substitutivity ... necessary conditions of the continuing self
            └─ Second-order distinction, view attribution (7.7) ... necessary conditions of the social person (by configuration assumption)
               └─ Class illusory constructs ... we, you (plural)
```


- **[Self-maintained filtering boundary](concepts.md#c47)**: a structure, maintained by one's own [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), that filters what can [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at oneself. This paper does not specify which kind of carrier, so this is written in general form; the cell membrane is an instance among living things. What it shapes is the [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17), and the [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17) is determined by dependence, not by [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (the definition of [own segment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c17)). So having a boundary such as a cell membrane does not imply that the cell has made a self-definition.
- **[Self-other discrimination](concepts.md#c48)**: [discriminating](concepts.md#c22) the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) that come back to oneself into those caused by oneself and those not caused by oneself. By the premise of 2.3, every [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) comes back to the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that made it; but the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) that come back to it carry no mark saying "this was caused by oneself".
- **[Cross-moment lumping](concepts.md#c49)**: grouping the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) caused by oneself at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) into one class. At the content layer, the researcher attributes it as upstream [identity](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c21).
- **[Substitutivity](concepts.md#c50)**: the content is still used when the self is not present as a [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30).

Calibration: [Self-other discrimination](concepts.md#c48): the reafference principle of von Holst and Mittelstaedt (1950); crickets suppressing their auditory response to their own chirps while chirping (Poulet and Hedwig, 2006); people cannot tickle themselves (Blakemore et al., 1998). That crickets have this mechanism shows that it is only a necessary condition. The continuing [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19): mirror self-recognition (Gallup, 1970); whether it can distinguish this is still disputed.

<note>

*There are three groups of necessary conditions. Without distinction, there is no construct that is the content of its distinction; when the content of a distinction only takes part in later actions and is not used to point at something, that content is not a construct. Without grouping things already drawn into a class, there is no construct of the "class of things" kind. To assume that it defines itself by distinction, engineering requires at least this: keeping a copy of its own movements and comparing it with the sensations that come back, so it can tell "I did this"; and being able to remember itself at different times and group them into one class, so there is a "continuing self". These latter ones are generalized from cases and can be overturned.*

*The "self ladder" is necessary conditions layer upon layer: first a self-maintained boundary, such as a cell membrane; then distinction; then telling self from others; then treating oneself at different times as the same one; then second-order capacity, and only then can it be assumed to be a social person; and only at the end come constructs like "we" and "you (plural)". Every layer is only a necessary condition, used to rule out, not to prove. Examples: crickets suppress their own sound while chirping, and people cannot tickle themselves; this is telling "what I did". Whether recognizing oneself in a mirror means there is a continuing self is still disputed.*

</note>

### 6.4 No Guarantee

**Conclusion**: Even with all the necessary conditions of points 1 and 2 of 6.3 present, the generation of [illusory constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is still [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: This paper does not write sufficient conditions (1.2): points 1 and 2 of 6.3 list necessary conditions, and no rule says that a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is generated when they are present; by the definition of [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02), the framework does not assert that it necessarily happens. Moreover, by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50) and the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) cannot be uniquely derived in advance; this paper has no further rule that fixes, within it, whether a given [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is made. Sources: the way of writing in 1.2; the definition of [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02); the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50); the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25).

Note: The content of a [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) is determined by the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) up to that time (the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18)). This does not conflict with this conclusion: "the history up to that time" includes the results of earlier [collapses](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c08); among them, the results of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) cannot, by the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), be uniquely derived in advance at an earlier point.

<note>

*Even with all conditions present, a construct is not guaranteed to arise, because this paper states only necessary conditions and no rule says that it must arise once they are present. Besides, the next step of something not locked in cannot be calculated in advance to begin with. The content drawn is determined by the history at that time, but the results in that history that were not locked in cannot be calculated earlier.*

</note>

### 6.5 Unconfirmability

**Conclusion**: That an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is generated inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02) to be confirmable from outside; on this, the judgments this paper gives from outside are only negations.

Derivation: [Distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is not in this paper's [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) (2.2); the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) concerned saying the name of some [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is a physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), from which it cannot be derived that it has a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of the same name inside (2.1); the researcher's classing this [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) as the subject's [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02), which by the definition of [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) may be misattributed. So confirming from outside that the subject has made a given [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02) (1.2).

The negative judgments given from outside are of two kinds: first, a mechanism is found proving that none of its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06); by 9.3 it is then not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is an act of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) (the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18)), so it has no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), and hence no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (6.1); second, it lacks one of the conditions listed in point 3 of 6.3, in [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) One or Two, and the [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04) does not hold for it (1.5); this kind negates the [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04), not the generation of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01).

Note: 6.4 and this conclusion are two different things. 6.4 is "whether it will be generated"; this one is "if it is generated, whether it can be seen".

<note>

*Even if a construct really did arise inside something, it is not guaranteed to be visible from outside, because the record catches only what it said and did, and saying a name does not mean having that meaning inside. What this paper gives is only ruling out: prove that it is not a Wo at all, and then it has no distinction and no construct that is the content of its distinction; or it lacks the conditions listed in Empirical Premise One or Two, and then it cannot be assumed to define itself by distinction. "Whether it will arise" and "if it arose, whether it can be seen" are two different things.*

</note>

### 6.6 Class Illusory Constructs

**Conclusion**: [Constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that are the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) all come from a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) that contains [lumping](concepts.md#c23) (5.1, 6.1); for example, this paper reads "I" as repeated [lumping](concepts.md#c23) of the [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) caused by oneself at different [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09), and reads "we" and "husband" as further [lumping](concepts.md#c23) of the results of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), that is, as [class illusory constructs](concepts.md#c10). The researcher's description is the repeated grouping of a large amount of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) into a few [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). At the engineering layer, what corresponds is repeated [compression](concepts.md#c31).

**Conclusion**: Reprocessing and recursion: if [representation](concepts.md#c34) A is indeed retained, or forms [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that can [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) again, it may take part in processing together with new [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) or another [representation](concepts.md#c34) B, forming [representation](concepts.md#c34) C; it may also be lost, distorted or not adopted. The whole process is still existing [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04), and no further generative law of "abstract creation" is added. Whether [compression](concepts.md#c31) is a pathway by which new [representations](concepts.md#c34) form from reprocessing existing [representations](concepts.md#c34) cannot be derived; this paper does not treat [compression](concepts.md#c31), [representation](concepts.md#c34) and [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) as synonyms, nor does it claim that [representations](concepts.md#c34) necessarily increase, are necessarily correct, or that complexity necessarily rises.

**Conclusion**: [Representations](concepts.md#c34) and [class illusory constructs](concepts.md#c10): when the content of a [representation](concepts.md#c34) is used to organize [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), and the object it points to is not given by the physical world, the researcher's attributing it as an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of some [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), or, when it is a further [lumping](concepts.md#c23) of the results of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), as that [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [class illusory construct](concepts.md#c10), is in either case a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) by the researcher, made on the basis of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), and may be mistaken (5.1).

<note>

*Constructs that are the content of distinction all come from a distinction that contains grouping; for example, "I" can be seen as grouping oneself at different times into one, again and again, and "we" and "husband" as grouping people already drawn into a class; a researcher's description is also grouping a heap of behavior into a few words. If a representation is kept, it may be processed together with new things and become a new representation; it may also be lost, distorted, or not used. All this is still the same old kind of round, with no extra rule of "creation". Whether compression is the road by which old representations are reprocessed into new ones cannot be derived. A researcher calling some representation a construct drawn by some thing itself, or a construct of the "class of things" kind, is in either case his judgment, and may be wrong.*

</note>

## 7. Question Three: Conditions of Use

### 7.1 Two Pathways of Use

**Conclusion**: There are two pathways for using an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01): inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the content of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) again through a [small loop](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c02) and participates in its later [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is carried by physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at other [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), possibly by way of other [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25).

Derivation: By 2.1, an [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) has two ways of appearing: in the researcher's description, and in actual [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). Inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that made it (the premise of 2.3). Between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), by point 3 of 4.3, what [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others is [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), not the act of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18).

Notes:
1. Between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), "use" is not the transfer of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). A name [arriving](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a receiver can be the receiver's material; if the receiver makes a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) with it, that is the receiver's own [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (6.2, note 1).
2. [Output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) carrying a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) can enter a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26), including spoken words (2.2), and then [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) from the container at other [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), including after the speaker is no longer present.
3. [Output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) carrying content can also come from [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) that are not [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), for example [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11). It has no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); on stated dimensions, when its [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the effect can be the same as that of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) with which a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) uses a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) (9.1, note 2).
4. A baker working alone does not say things in terms, and writes the steps in terms only when teaching; a person climbing alone does not say "that tree", and says it only with a companion (calibration of 4.2). When nothing is said, it cannot be derived from outside whether this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has this [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) inside; what can be determined is only whether it has appeared in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (4.3, note 5).

<note>

*Constructs are used in two ways: on oneself, where content once drawn comes back and affects what follows; and between people, where it passes over through what is said aloud or written down.
