What passes over is not the construct itself; what the other draws from it is the other's own affair. Words can also be stored in a container first and still reach others after the speaker is gone. Artificial intelligence can also output such words, and once they reach people, in some respects the effect can be the same as when a person says them, but there is no distinction behind them.*

</note>

### 7.2 Necessary Conditions

1. **Use between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)** (conclusion, by point 3 of 4.3 and the definition of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10)): if no physical [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) carrying the [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the receiver, the [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) does not have the [function](concepts.md#c18) of [transmission between Wo](concepts.md#c21).
2. **Use inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) when [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27) is not present** ([empirical premise](concepts.md#c01)): without retention across [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) (belonging to [memory capacity](concepts.md#c15)), the content of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) cannot take part in organizing [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) when the [stimulus](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c30) is not present (Section 10, [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Three).

Note: Point 1 is derived from definitions. Point 2 follows the form of [empirical prohibition](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c51), stating only that without it, it [never occurs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c01).

<note>

*To pass content that points at something between people, there must be something said or done, and it must really reach the other. To use a construct when the thing is not in front of one, one must at least be able to remember; this latter point is generalized from cases.*

</note>

### 7.3 Templates and Their Execution

**Conclusion**: From obtaining the same [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), it cannot be derived that those who obtain it turn [arrived material](concepts.md#c28) into [representations](concepts.md#c34) in the same way. What the researcher can register as "the parties execute the template consistently" lies only in the parties' [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); if these [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at one another within stated dimensions and time windows and repeatedly follow on compatibly, then by the definition of [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) this is [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09).

Derivation:
1. A [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) is content [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) in a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26). The container is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), and its content does not change by itself (the definitions of [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) and [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26)).
2. Turning new [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) into the [representations](concepts.md#c34) the [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) uses is the obtainer's own [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04). If the obtainer is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it participates in this [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) (the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)), and the [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) is only one item in it; two [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) obtaining the same [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) may differ in the rest of their [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13). Moreover, by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50), the obtainer is assumed not to be [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) in this [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04); by the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), its next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) cannot be uniquely derived in advance. So from obtaining the same [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), it cannot be derived that the two [compressions](concepts.md#c31) are the same.
3. A [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) can also [explicitly list](concepts.md#c09) a description of a way of [compression](concepts.md#c31). But applying this description is again the obtainer's own [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04), and point 2 applies likewise.
4. By the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope of 2.2, the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) contains only the [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and cannot directly confirm the ways in which they turn [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) into [representations](concepts.md#c34). If their [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) applying the [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at one another within stated dimensions and time windows and repeatedly follow on compatibly, then by the definition of [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09), this is [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09).

Sources: the definitions of [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26), [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09); the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50); the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25); the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope of 2.2.

Notes:
1. From obtaining a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), it also cannot be derived that its scope of application, that is, the set of [cases](concepts.md#c24) on which it is [commutative](concepts.md#c55), is obtained: if the scope is not [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09), it is not in the container; if it is [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09), by the definition of [commutative within a range](concepts.md#c42) it is still only a claim. Calibration: Newton's laws are often taught as a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), while the condition of application that speeds are far below the speed of light is often not written out.
2. If "resonance maintains consistency of execution" means that [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) makes the parties' [compressions](concepts.md#c31) tend to agree, that is a causal claim, and cannot be derived. This conclusion says only: the consistency the researcher can register lies only in [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); when these [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at one another within stated dimensions and time windows and repeatedly follow on compatibly, it is [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09).
3. If the obtainer is a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) at every step, point 2 does not apply: when two [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) obtain the same [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), their [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) are fixed by constraints and can be the same; reading these two [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as the same [compression](concepts.md#c31) is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads them (Proposition Five). So when a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) is passed on, the [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) of [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) can be consistent; the consistency of execution by [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Calibration: two people take the same recipe; how each applies "a pinch of salt" and "fry until golden" depends on each person's whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); two calculators of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) running the same program, with the same input, give the same [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). A baker writes the steps as terms and hands them to an apprentice; the apprentice, following them, still has to practice again and again and compare again and again with the master and the results before making the same bread: the terms passed on the [inference](concepts.md#c36), and execution has to be joined up in practice.

<note>

*Getting the same rules does not mean everyone follows them the same way, because each person applies them with their own experience. The "everyone does it consistently" a researcher can see lies only in what everyone produces; only when these things reach one another and keep fitting together within stated dimensions and time windows is it called resonance. Getting the rules also does not mean getting their scope; for example, Newton's laws often do not say "speeds must be far below the speed of light". Machines are different: two identical machines running the same program can give the same result; the one who reads it as the same calculation is still the one looking at it. With the same recipe, two people cook different dishes; an apprentice who gets the master's terms still has to practice again and again to join up.*

</note>

### 7.4 No Guarantee

**Conclusion**: Even with all the necessary conditions of 7.2 present, use is still [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: As in 6.4.

<note>

*Even with all conditions present, a construct will not necessarily be used. The reason is as in 6.4.*

</note>

### 7.5 Unconfirmability

**Conclusion**: Whether a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is used inside a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) to organize [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02) to be confirmable from outside; between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), what the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) contains is only the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that carries it.

Derivation: As in 6.5; 2.1.

<note>

*Whether something is using a certain construct inside is not guaranteed to be visible from outside; between people, what can be recorded is only that sentence or that document.*

</note>

### 7.6 Comparison: Coordination Without Constructs, and Use Through Templates

[Culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) is the case in which [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) can follow on compatibly with one another even without [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01); when [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) carrying [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) into a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26), it can become a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14); a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) is one possible way in which [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are used between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

1. **[Culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) is not conditioned on [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18)** (conclusion): by the definition of [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09), [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) is conditioned on [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10); by the definition of [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13), [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) is the researcher's name for [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) appearing in a network; by the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), whether a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has nothing to do with any [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). So [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) is not conditioned on the participants' making [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), still less on [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), [templates](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) or [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01). From there being [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), it does not follow that there is [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13).
2. **[Explicit listing](concepts.md#c09) is not limited to writing** (conclusion): by 2.2, the [explicit listing](concepts.md#c09) of a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) can be writing down, and can also be speaking aloud, broadcasting or screening. So language falls on both sides at once: the parts that are said explicitly or recorded, for example the words in a sentence and rules stated orally, can be [templates](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), and whether they are is judged by the definition of [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) and the criterion the researcher specifies; the usages no one states, which follow on compatibly through repeated speaking, are named [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) by the researcher if, under the [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), dimensions and time windows the researcher specifies, they are [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) in a network; a feel for language, collocation and intonation are examples of such usages. By the definition of [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) is not a layer of [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13).
3. **The [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) cannot reveal the inside** (conclusion): from there being a [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06), it cannot be derived that the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that [explicitly listed](concepts.md#c09) it has a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) inside: by the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope of 2.2, there is no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06); by 2.1, from an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) it cannot be derived that the speaker has a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of the same name inside.

Calibration: a baker and apprentices working again and again in the same kitchen, joining up again and again, are on the side of [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) if, under the specified [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), dimensions and time windows, this is [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09); the steps and terms he writes down can be on the side of [templates](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14).

<note>

*Culture does not require anyone to have constructs: as long as what people do keeps fitting together within the specified scope, the researcher can call it culture. A template is one way of putting constructs into a container and passing them to others. Language has both: rules stated explicitly or written down can be templates; the feel for language that no one states, joined up through speaking again and again, is called culture by the researcher if, within the specified scope, it is resonance. A set of rules in the record does not mean the writer has that construct inside.*

</note>

### 7.7 First Order and Second Order

**First order is not a gear** (conclusion): by the definition of [first-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c24), [first-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c24) acts according to the distinction between a view and [what is seen](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c27), but does not make this distinction. By the definition of [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04), [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04) is not conditioned on [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01); by the definitions of [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and point 1 of 7.6, [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) and [culture](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c13) are not conditioned on [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). So neither [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04) nor [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) is conditioned on [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01); the definitions do not exclude a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that has only [first-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c24) from [learning](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c04), and from forming [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) with other [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) through repeated [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [outcome feedback](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c05).

**The antecedent of [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01)** (conclusion): by the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), the material of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is only the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) before it that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it, and it itself is not among it. [Second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) takes one of one's own [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) as its object, so that one is a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) already made and already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it; this point follows the premise of 2.3: a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) itself before the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09). This paper adds no further condition of "remembering the content of that distinction": by the definition of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), the [writing](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c29) left by a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) already made is not erased, and still participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) takes it as object, and the definition of [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) requires only taking it as object, not being able to name its original content.

Calibration: a person who does not remember when or why he began to fear dogs can still say "my fear of dogs is only a view".

**[Social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03)** (by the definition of [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03)): a [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03) is a biological person with [second-order capacity](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c02); calling a biological person a [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03) is done under the [configuration assumption](../paper_ae79e938_76e1_4bf9_b2ab_995c03e16c9e/concepts.md#c01). Calibration: ordinary adults widely use [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) such as "we" and "you (plural)"; an empirical reason of this kind is only a reason for adopting this configuration, not a proof of second-order inner [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18).

**Relation to use**: apart from [we](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c04) and [plural you](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c05), which by definition are used by a [social person](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c03) and require the capacities for [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) and [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02), the framework does not assert that [second-order distinction](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c01) or [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) is a necessary or sufficient condition of the use of [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), nor the reverse; and what [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02) requires in engineering, cannot be derived.

<note>

*Something with only first-order distinction is not a machine part: the definitions do not exclude that it can still learn, and can keep fitting together with others, only it does not turn back to see its own thoughts as "only thoughts". To look back at a particular thought, that thought must first have come back to oneself; but there is no need to remember what was thought at the time; for example, without remembering when one began to fear dogs, one can still say "fearing dogs is only my view". Ordinary adults are called social persons because they often say "we" and "you (plural)"; this is a reason, not a proof. Apart from constructs such as "we" and "you (plural)", which are used by social persons and require second-order distinction and view attribution, the framework does not assert which of second-order capacity and the use of constructs is a condition of the other.*

</note>

---

## 8. Question Four: After Use

### 8.1 Consequences Inside a Wo

**Conclusion**: When a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is used to organize [inference](concepts.md#c36), if the result of the [inference](concepts.md#c36) is [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41), then on every registered [case](concepts.md#c24) the [inference result](concepts.md#c37) and the [later representation](concepts.md#c33) are the same within the [allowed error](concepts.md#c56); if it is not [commutative](concepts.md#c55), there are [cases](concepts.md#c24) that do not agree. When the disagreeing material [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), it participates in its later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); whether this [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) therefore ceases to be used is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: the criterion of 5.3; the definition of [outcome feedback](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c05) (consequences are brought back through later [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10)); the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); the definition of [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02); the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50); the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25).

Note: "Interrupted" and "eliminated" are only metaphors, with no purpose, and not guaranteed to happen.

<note>

*When a construct is used to infer, if the inference fits, what is received later agrees with it; if it does not fit, there will be times of disagreement. The disagreeing experience still affects the person, but whether he therefore stops using this construct is not guaranteed. Saying a construct is "eliminated" is only a metaphor.*

</note>

### 8.2 Continued Use Is Not Conditioned on Being Commutative

**Conclusion**: From a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) not being [commutative](concepts.md#c55), it cannot be derived that it ceases to be used; when the [inference rules](concepts.md#c44) that use this [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are [self-consistent](concepts.md#c45) but not [commutative](concepts.md#c55), this [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) can also go on being used.

Derivation: Being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) is not among the conditions of use listed in 7.2. Disagreeing [later material](concepts.md#c30) still [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and still participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) (the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)); but by 8.1, whether the [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) therefore ceases to be used is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02). Moreover, by Proposition Three, [comparison](concepts.md#c38) reaches only [cases](concepts.md#c24) that have [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10): if the [inference](concepts.md#c36) of a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) has never been [compared](concepts.md#c38) with later [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), its not being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) does not appear in this [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) in the form of a [comparison](concepts.md#c38). By Proposition Six, [self-consistency](concepts.md#c45) does not imply being [commutative](concepts.md#c55), and being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) does not imply [self-consistency](concepts.md#c45). Sources: 7.2; 8.1; Propositions Three and Six; the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

<note>

*A construct not fitting what arrives later does not mean people stop using it; when the set of rules using it makes internal sense yet does not fit, this construct may also be used on and on. Using a construct does not require it to fit; and if it has never been compared with what came later, "not fitting" never even appears in that person's experience.*

</note>

### 8.3 Being Commutative and Continued Use

**Conclusion**: The framework does not assert any relation, beyond that of 8.2, between being [commutative](concepts.md#c55) and whether a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) goes on being used; for example, whether, in domains where [outcome feedback](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c05) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) frequently, [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that are not [commutative](concepts.md#c55) are less often kept in use, cannot be derived.

<note>

*Whether constructs that do not fit are less often kept in use, for example in domains where consequences are often seen at once, cannot be derived by this paper.*

</note>

### 8.4 Consequences Between Wo

**Conclusion**: [Constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at other [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) through [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), participate in their later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), and may become material for their further [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). On the basis of these registered [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) and later [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), the researcher may draw an [inside outside difference](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c09) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06); on the basis of those among them whose content is a classification, may draw a [claimed boundary](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c10); and comparing two [check windows](../paper_d244789a_9261_4a0d_a98a_feb8db56b511/concepts.md#c03), may record the difference between them as a [boundary shift](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c11). Whether any of these appears, and which way it goes, cannot be derived.

Derivation: By 7.1, [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are carried between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) by [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); by the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) participates in its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); by the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), the material of a [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is only the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that has already [arrived](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it, so these [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) may become material for the receiver's further [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). By the definitions of [inside outside difference](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c09), [claimed boundary](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c10) and [boundary shift](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c11), all three are [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) the researcher makes on the basis of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06), so whether they can be drawn depends on which [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) are registered in the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06), and on the [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) the researcher states. By the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50), the receiver is assumed not to be [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) at these [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09); by the [Law of the Black Box](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c25), what [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) it later produces cannot be uniquely derived in advance, so which way it goes cannot be derived.

Note: Science can be described as such a network: it produces [templates](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14) [commutative over registered cases](concepts.md#c41), and eliminates those that are not [commutative](concepts.md#c55) by [outcome feedback](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c05); scope and execution are maintained in the users' [resonance](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c09) (7.3). This is a way of describing, not a proposition derived by this paper.

<note>

*Constructs pass to others through what is said aloud or written down, affect them, and may become material for their drawing again. In the record, a researcher may see: the same thing done differently toward insiders and outsiders (inside outside difference); someone stating or writing down a boundary (claimed boundary); comparing two stretches of time, the boundary having moved (boundary shift). Which of these will appear cannot be derived. Science can also be seen as such a web: it produces rules that fit, and eliminates those that do not by their consequences; but this is only a way of describing.*

</note>

### 8.5 No Guarantee

**Conclusion**: Which consequence follows after use is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02).

Derivation: As in 6.4.

<note>

*What will happen after a construct is used is not guaranteed.*

</note>

### 8.6 The Network Layer

After [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) are used among many [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) through [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), how the complexity a network can sustain changes is not derived from the capacities of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) alone: population, division of labor, resources, the maintenance of [signal containers](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) and [templates](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), and the density of [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) all take part. This paper does not deal with it.

<note>

*After many people are using constructs among themselves, how complex a structure the whole society can hold up cannot be derived just from each person's capacity; population, division of labor, resources, the upkeep of documents and rules, and how densely people interact all matter. This paper does not discuss it.*

</note>

---

## 9. Control Group: Current Artificial Intelligence

This section is the control group for the whole paper: [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) has [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12), its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), and the researcher can read these [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as [compressions](concepts.md#c31) and [compare](concepts.md#c38) whether they are [commutative](concepts.md#c55), yet under the logical-state [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) it is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16). It belongs to none of the four questions, and is used to test the answers to each.

<note>

*This section uses today's artificial intelligence as a comparison: it has engineering capacities, and what it calculates can be read as "compressed rightly, inferred accurately", yet under this paper's cut it is not a Wo. It is used to check the answers to the four questions above.*

</note>

### 9.1 Simulation

**Definition**:

> **[Simulation](concepts.md#c51)**: under a stated [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) and dimensions, a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) that is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) whose [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), by a criterion the researcher states, cannot be distinguished from the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of some [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) or some class of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).
>
> **[Functional equivalence](concepts.md#c52)**: on stated dimensions, after this [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25)'s [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at other [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25), the effects it has are the same as those of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). These effects include: participating in the receiver's [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06); supplying or removing an [antecedent](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c08) of some [path](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c07) of the receiver; being read as carrying a content; and having as its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) that the researcher reads as [commutative](concepts.md#c55) [inference](concepts.md#c36).

**Conclusion**: [Current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); on certain dimensions stated by the researcher, if the effects stated depend only on the content of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) on these dimensions, two [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that are the same on these dimensions have the same effects when they [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the same [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), and in this sense it can be [functionally equivalent](concepts.md#c52) to a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

**[Empirical premise](concepts.md#c01)**: on certain dimensions stated by the researcher, the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) cannot be distinguished from human [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) (Section 10, [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Four); this paper records humans as [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) (Section 10), so on these dimensions, it is a [simulation](concepts.md#c51) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

Derivation:
1. **Not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)**: by 9.3, under the logical-state [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), none of its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06).
2. **Can be [functionally equivalent](concepts.md#c52)**: by [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Four, on certain dimensions stated by the researcher, its [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) cannot be distinguished from human [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50) and this paper's setting (Section 10), humans are recorded as [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16). By the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) all participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), regardless of which class of [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) the source is. So if the effects stated by the researcher depend only on the content of the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) on these dimensions, then when two [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) that are the same on these dimensions [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the same [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), they are the same in these effects, regardless of which class of [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) the source is. By Proposition Five, a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) whose [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) need not make [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); what reads the [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as [inference](concepts.md#c36), and judges whether it is [commutative](concepts.md#c55), is the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads it. So on these dimensions, a [simulation](concepts.md#c51) and a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) can be [functionally equivalent](concepts.md#c52).
3. **The judgment does not rest on [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28)**: the [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) does not contain the kind of [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04). So judging that it is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) rests on its design (9.2), not on [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). The same holds for humans: humans are recorded as [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50) and this paper's setting (Section 10), not by [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28).

Notes:
1. [Functional equivalence](concepts.md#c52) holds only on stated dimensions, and is not guaranteed on all dimensions. On at least two dimensions the two are not guaranteed to be the same: its [locked-in rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c25), when its constraints are determined only by [structure](concepts.md#c07) and state, can be passed on completely by copying, while for [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) with different earlier [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) it cannot be derived that this is possible (9.6); and when copies of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) that are [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) obtain the same [template](../paper_2ccbf179_58cf_4851_bd72_5fa886e4b2c3/concepts.md#c14), their [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) can be identical, while the execution of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) being the same is [not guaranteed](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c02) (7.3).
2. **[Current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) and [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01)**: by the definition of [illusory construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) is the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), or a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) used in description and [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) has no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), so it has no [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01) that is the content of its own [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). Its [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), for example names, "we", or a patch on a map, can be read by the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that reads them as carrying some content; these [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and can become material for [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) to make [distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and form [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01). In the use of [constructs](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), on the stated dimensions, [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) can be a [functionally equivalent](concepts.md#c52) [simulation](concepts.md#c51): the effect of its [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) after [arriving](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) can be the same as that of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) with which a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) uses a [construct](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c01), with no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) behind it.

<note>

*"Simulation" means: something that is not a Wo produces what, by an agreed standard, cannot be told apart from what a Wo produces. "Functional equivalence" means: when its output reaches others, it has the same effect as a Wo's output, for example affecting others, letting some path of theirs go through or not, being read as meaningful words, or being read as an accurate inference.*

*Today's artificial intelligence is not a Wo, but if the stated effects depend only on what its output contains on certain dimensions, outputs that are the same on those dimensions have the same effect when they reach the same person, and in that sense it can be the same as a person; on some tests its output cannot be told apart from a person's, which is a measured result that new tests may overturn. Since on these tests output alone cannot tell them apart, saying it is not a Wo rests on how it is designed. The same goes for humans: saying a human is a Wo is an assumption, not something seen from output. Artificial intelligence itself has no construct that is the content of its own distinction, but the names and the "we" it says can, once they reach people, become material for people's distinctions; the effect is the same as when a person says them, yet there is no distinction behind them.*

</note>

### 9.2 The Cut on Which the Judgment Rests

**Premise**: This paper records: under the [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) of logical state, for current digital artificial intelligence designed on a Turing-machine basis, every [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is either a [locked-in round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c25) or a [random round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c26).

**Premise**: This paper records: the carrier of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) can perform the kinds of acts and retention measured by the five [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12): distinguishing and handling input differences, performing [operations](concepts.md#c08) on [representations](concepts.md#c34), retaining and retrieving earlier states, re-representing with limited resources, and taking part in [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) with existing [structure](concepts.md#c07).

Notes:
1. This judgment rests on design facts, not on [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). The [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) is stated by the [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19) who makes the judgment.
2. By the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), whether a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) has nothing to do with any [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). So whether the physical carrier of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) this paper does not judge; the conclusions of this section all hold under this [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23).
3. For other physical bases, the judgment is made separately.

<note>

*This paper's judgment is: cut by the states of program logic, every step of today's digital artificial intelligence is either calculated rigidly by rules or drawn by random numbers. This rests on its design, not on what it says. Whether its chips, circuits and other physical carriers are Wo this paper does not judge; for another physical basis, the judgment must be made anew.*

</note>

### 9.3 Not a Wo Under This Cut

**Conclusion**: A [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) none of whose [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06) is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16). So under this [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

Derivation: By the definition of [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06), the [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) when it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) is a [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) in which the whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), that is, a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06). By the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) at least at some [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09), and the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it all participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), so at least one [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06). By 9.2, none of the [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is a [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06). Sources: the definitions of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) and [decision](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c06); 9.2.

Notes:
1. This conclusion reads "the history that arrives at it all participates in weight" in the definition of [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) as holding at every [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09); "this participation may gradually fade, and once it has faded it is no longer a Wo" in the definition supports this reading.
2. [Lock-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) does not cut off participation: at a [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) when a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) there is only one result, and the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at it still participates in its later [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06). For example, a person tied up cannot move, but the experience of being tied still participates in his [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) after he is untied. So from a [presentation](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c04) sometimes being [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), it does not follow that it is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).
3. That [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) rests on its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) that are not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) all being [random rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c26), not on its sometimes being [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12). The two pure cases listed in the upstream [configuration assumption](../paper_ae79e938_76e1_4bf9_b2ab_995c03e16c9e/concepts.md#c01), namely that the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) can fall out only of [locked-in rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c25) or only of [random rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c26), and the case mixing the two, all follow from this point.

<note>

*A Wo has at least one round of the kind where "the whole of its experience is at work and the result still cannot be calculated rigidly"; this is called a decision. Today's artificial intelligence never has one, so under this cut it is not a Wo. Being tied up and unable to move does not mean not being a Wo, because the experience of being tied still affects him afterwards; artificial intelligence is not a Wo because whenever it is not calculated rigidly, it is only drawing random numbers.*

</note>

### 9.4 Its Place in the Classification of Nodes

**Conclusion**: Under this [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), with respect to a given [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09): if [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) is [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) at every step from this [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) on, it is a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) at this [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09); if at this [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) or afterwards it still has [moments](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) at which it is not [locked in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), it is a [random source](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c27) at this [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09).

Derivation: By the definition of [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25), there are only three classes of [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25): [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) and [random source](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c27); by 9.3, it is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); it is then classified by the definitions of [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) and [random source](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c27).

Note: By the definitions of these two classes, they cannot be the object of an [impression](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45), and [cognitive obscuration](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c35) has no content for a [signal container](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26). So statistical estimates made of the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) are, under this [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), not what the upstream calls [impressions](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c45).

<note>

*There are only three kinds of node: Wo, container and random source. Artificial intelligence is not a Wo, so look at it: take a given moment: if from that moment on everything is calculated rigidly, it is a container at that moment; if at that moment or afterwards it still draws random numbers, it is a random source at that moment. So statistics on the output of artificial intelligence do not count as what the upstream calls an "impression".*

</note>

### 9.5 Outputs Can Be Similar, and on Arriving at a Wo They Likewise Participate in Weight

**Conclusion**: On the dimensions stated in [Empirical Premise](concepts.md#c01) Four, [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) can produce [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) similar to those of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); when these [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), they participate in the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) like any other [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10). On the stated dimensions, by [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) alone, a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and its [simulation](concepts.md#c51) cannot be told apart (9.1).

Derivation: By the definitions of [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), the [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) all participates in [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06), regardless of which class of [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) the source is. The [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) does not contain the kind of [round](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04); by the definition of [simulation](concepts.md#c51), on the stated dimensions, the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of a [simulation](concepts.md#c51) cannot be distinguished from that of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16).

Note: Humans are recorded as [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) by the [Application Assumption](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c50) and this paper's setting (Section 10), not by [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28). The researcher, or a person assumed to be a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19) under the [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04), taking the [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) as "its view" is making a [view attribution](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c02), which may be misattributed. The [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) of [current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) can describe a [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23), but that is content produced by [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05); the one who adopts and states it is the researcher.

<note>

*What artificial intelligence says, once it reaches people, affects them just as what others say does. By the agreed standard, looking only at what was said, a Wo cannot be told from a simulation. The researcher, or a person assumed to be a "self", taking its words as "its view" is that person's own attribution, and may be wrong. It can write out a cut, but the one who adopts and states this cut is the researcher.*

</note>

### 9.6 Ways of Processing Can Be Copied

**Conclusion**: Between [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) whose constraints are determined only by [structure](concepts.md#c07) and state, the way of processing [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) can be passed on completely by copying the state; between two [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) with different earlier [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), it cannot be derived that this can be done.

Derivation:
1. The next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) of a [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) is fixed by constraints (the definition of [lock-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12)). For two [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07), if their constraints are determined only by [structure](concepts.md#c07) and state, then when they are in the same state, the same [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) produces the same result; that constraints are determined only by [structure](concepts.md#c07) and state is an assumption about the [structure](concepts.md#c07) of the machine, not derived from definitions; that the state can be fully read out and copied into another machine of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) is also an assumption about the [structure](concepts.md#c07) of the machine.
2. By the definitions of [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) only extends forward, and the whole [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) that [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) participates in its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06). So any [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) only adds one more item to the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)'s [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); when it is still a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) after this [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), its earlier [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) still participates in its [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06). The result in point 1 rests on assumptions about the machine's [structure](concepts.md#c07); this paper makes no such assumption about [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16), so if the earlier [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) of two [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) differ, whether [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) can make the ways they process [arrivals](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) the same cannot be derived.

Sources: the definitions of [lock-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12), [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13) and [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16); the setting for machines listed in Section 10.

Notes:
1. Between machines of different [structure](concepts.md#c07), the content must first be turned into a form the other can use; the researcher reads this conversion as a [compression](concepts.md#c31), which is not guaranteed to be complete.
2. With random [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04), if each of the two copies produces its own result and they do not share one random outcome, their single results can differ. If the [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) of such a [node](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) is determined only by its state and this [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), then when the two copies, while their states are still the same, receive the same [arrival](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10), their [weight](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c06) distributions are the same; this is an assumption about the [structure](concepts.md#c07) of the machine, not derived from definitions.
3. By point 3 of 4.3, what [arrives](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at others is [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28), not the act of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18); this conclusion shows that between [locked-in](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c12) [nodes](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c25) of the same [structure](concepts.md#c07) whose constraints are determined only by [structure](concepts.md#c07) and state, the way of processing can be passed on by copying the state, not through [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28); between [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) with different earlier [histories](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13), it cannot be derived that this can be done. Note 3 of 7.3 cites this conclusion.

<note>

*Two identical machines, as long as their rules depend only on structure and state, with the state copied over entirely, will process whatever they receive afterwards in the same way. People are different: however much you tell someone, it only adds one more item to his experience; as long as he is still a Wo, his earlier experience still affects him. This paper makes no assumption about people like the machines' "depends only on structure and state", so whether talking could make one person a copy of another, this paper cannot derive. Between different machines the format must first be converted, and conversion may lose things; with random numbers, if each copy draws its own, single results can differ; for the probabilities to be the same, one must first assume that the machine's probabilities depend only on its state and what it has just received.*

</note>

### 9.7 Having Engineering Capacities Does Not Imply Distinction

**Conclusion**: [Current artificial intelligence](concepts.md#c11) can perform the acts and retention measured by the five [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12) (9.2), its [rounds](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c04) are [computation](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05), and the researcher can read these [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) as [compression](concepts.md#c31) and [inference](concepts.md#c36) (Proposition Five), and [compare](concepts.md#c38), over the registered [cases](concepts.md#c24), whether they are [commutative](concepts.md#c55); yet under this [cut](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c23) it is not a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) and has no [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18). So from [computations](../paper_fb0addca_42b6_4884_aa18_438c695747ab/concepts.md#c05) being read as [compression](concepts.md#c31) and [inference](concepts.md#c36), or from having [engineering capacities](concepts.md#c12), it cannot be derived that there is [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18).

Derivation: Proposition Five; 9.3; the definition of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) ([distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) is an act of a [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16)).

<note>

*What artificial intelligence calculates can be read as compressed rightly and inferred accurately, but it is not a Wo and has no distinction. So from having engineering capacities, it cannot be derived that there is distinction.*

</note>

### 9.8 Engineering Threshold and Observer Recognition Threshold

**Definition**: [Engineering threshold](concepts.md#c53): with a specified architecture and resources, whether a certain kind of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) can be produced stably under specified conditions. [Observer recognition threshold](concepts.md#c54): when outsiders begin to label this kind of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) as "intelligent", "understanding" or "having [second-order capacity](../paper_08383b0a_1167_4efb_9b89_dc8a71afd5ff/concepts.md#c02)".

**Conclusion**: That an [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) meets the criterion does not license inferring back to inner [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18), understanding or consciousness (9.5). The framework does not assert that crossing one of the thresholds means crossing the other.

Calibration: commonly, trained models run with existing parameters and are not updated in each conversation; intermediate states and the distribution of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) still change with input.

<note>

*The "engineering threshold" asks: given some architecture and resources, can a certain kind of output be produced stably? The "observer recognition threshold" asks: from when do outsiders start saying it "is intelligent" or "understands"? Output meeting a standard cannot be used to infer back that there is distinction or understanding inside. The framework does not assert that crossing one threshold means crossing the other. Today's common models do not change their parameters with each conversation when used, but their internal states and outputs still change with input.*

</note>

---

## 10. Premises, Empirical Premises and Failure Conditions

### 10.1 Premises

Premises are this paper's settings, not claims that can be overturned:

- The researcher has no position outside [history](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c13); the researcher is a [self](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c19) (1.4).
- The [Self-Configuration Assumption](concepts.md#c04) (1.5).
- [Distinctions](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) and [outputs](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) [arrive](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c10) at the [Wo](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c16) that made them before the next [moment](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c09) (2.3).
- The reading that [signal containers](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c26) are not limited to writing (2.2).
- The [record](../paper_81952268_47b1_4d8c_9605_270c42646693/concepts.md#c06) scope: only physical changes of [output](../paper_b9df3b68_23ee_4839_965b_ed0e6b283ee6/concepts.md#c28) are registered, not the content of [distinction](../paper_37b3643a_dc40_4463_8b36_4f4594acd986/concepts.md#c18) (2.2).
- [Definitional Isolation and Renaming Invariance](concepts.md#c06) (2.1).
- Limited reception (3.1).
